### [256] [Which LLM is Best for Translating Natural Language Goals to PDDL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18731)
*Tomas Balyo,Lukas Chrpa,G. Michael Youngblood*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了当前LLM能否将游戏测试者用非正式自然语言表达的测试目标可靠地翻译为PDDL目标，用于经典规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动规划中，人类意图与机器执行之间存在鸿沟：PDDL等形式语言表达目标对非专家不友好，因此需要评估LLM作为自然语言到PDDL翻译桥梁的可靠性。

Method: 设计了一个精心构建的提示模板（基于迭代实验经验），在真实、领域特定的基准上，从正确性、速度和错误倾向三方面系统评估六个当代最先进LLM。

Result: 所有模型正确率均超过92%；Gemini 2.5 Flash准确率最高（96%）且假阳性最低，GPT-4.1响应速度最快。但模型间存在显著性能差异，失败主要源于语言歧义和领域表示的限制。

Conclusion: LLM在连接自然语言目标与自动规划流水线方面取得显著进展，但仍存在持续差距，尚不能成为完全稳健的桥梁。

Abstract: Bridging the gap between human intent and machine execution remains a challenge in automated planning, where expressing goals in formal languages like PDDL restricts accessibility to non-experts. This paper empirically evaluates whether current Large Language Models (LLMs) can reliably translate natural language testing goals, written in informal language by video game testers, into well-formed PDDL targets suitable for classical planning. We present a carefully designed prompt template, integrating insights from iterative experimentation, aimed at maximizing both accuracy and response coherence from multiple state-of-the-art LLMs. Six contemporary models are systematically assessed on correctness, speed, and error tendencies using real-world, domain-specific benchmarks. All models demonstrate high correctness, exceeding 92\%, with Gemini 2.5 Flash achieving the highest accuracy at 96\% and the lowest incidence of false positives, while GPT-4.1 leads in response speed. Despite these advances, critical distinctions exist in model performance, and occasional failures arise from language ambiguity and limitations in domain representation. Our analysis underscores both the significant progress and ongoing gaps in enabling LLMs to act as robust bridges between natural language objectives and automated planning pipelines.

</details>


### [257] [Clueing up LLMs with Tool-Augmented Deductive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18736)
*Rebecca Ansell,Autumn Toney-Wails*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用基于文本的多智能体Clue桌游环境评测LLM的多步演绎推理，提出结构化的可能性矩阵工具增强方法，将隐式游戏状态转为显式可能性表示，卸载记忆与推理约束，并与基线对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长交互中进行逻辑一致的演绎推理仍具挑战，需整合多步证据、保持前后一致并随新约束更新信念，因此需要合适测试环境与增强方法。

Method: 构建文本版多智能体Clue环境；使用GPT-4o-mini与Gemini-2.5-Flash各3个智能体进行回合制对局建立基线；提出工具增强方法，用结构化可能性矩阵从推理日志中提取隐式游戏状态，编码长轮记忆与演绎约束。

Result: 建立重复对局基线性能，并对比工具增强方法，评估其对推理质量与任务成功率的支持作用。

Conclusion: 工具增强通过显式化状态与卸载记忆推理任务，有望提升自主智能体在策略推理环境中的演绎推理表现。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in large language models (LLMs), performing logically consistent deductive reasoning over extended interactions remains challenging. Tasks that require integrating evidence across multiple reasoning steps, maintaining consistency with prior inferences, and updating beliefs under new constraints can surface limitations in current models while providing a useful testbed for evaluating reasoning enhancements. In this paper, we implement a text-based, multi-agent version of the classic board game Clue as an environment to evaluate multi-step, agentic deductive reasoning. In this setting, agents must infer hidden information from a sequence of observations, maintain consistency across turns, and reason over an evolving set of logical constraints. We instantiate six LLM-based agents (GPT-4o-mini and Gemini-2.5-Flash) as players that engage in turn-based gameplay; using three agents per model family, we establish baseline performance across repeated games. We then introduce a tool-augmented approach in which a structured possibility matrix converts implicit game state from generated reasoning logs into an explicit representation of remaining possibilities. The possibility matrix encodes extended-turn memory and deductive constraints, offloading these tasks from the agent. We compare this approach against the baseline to evaluate how tool augmentation supports reasoning quality and task success for autonomous agents in a strategic reasoning environment.

</details>


### [258] [Version- and Scope-Aware Question Answering over Normative Documents: A Deployed System and an End-to-End Evaluation at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18769)
*Liuyin Wang,Shuaipeng Jin,Jiwei Shi,Jensen Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文评估"上传文档直接提问"的托管检索默认范式，与通过显式规则先解决版本和适用范围问题的受治理系统对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范文档问答依赖单一文本之外的信息：检索文档是否为现行有效版本、是否适用于涉案司法辖区/主体/日期、以及规范主张能否溯源。托管检索服务降低了入门工程成本，使"上传即问"成为默认做法，需评估其可靠性。

Method: 在约73,000份候选规范文档的生产部署上，采用来自公开基准的200道分层抽样问题（每题含金标源文档），抽样规则在读取任何系统输出或分数前发布。对比托管服务与受治理系统——后者在生成前通过显式规则解决版本与适用范围问题。

Result: 受治理系统总分97.7，托管服务88.1，差距9.6分（基于未四舍五入均值）。问题集、两系统的答案文本、分数及复现基准统计的脚本均已公开。

Conclusion: 受治理配置自2026年1月起作为商业产品运营，服务1,126名注册用户，客户包括智谱AI和乐城健康；至2026年4月中旬达到每工作日约10万次调用。

Abstract: Correctly answering a question grounded in normative documents often depends on information outside any single passage: whether the retrieved document is the version currently in force; whether it applies to the jurisdiction, subject (such as an institution or applicant), and date at issue; and whether each normative claim can be traced to its supporting source text. Hosted retrieval services have substantially lowered the engineering cost of building an initial system over such corpora, making "upload the documents and ask" a common default. We evaluate this default on approximately 73,000 candidate normative documents supplied to a production deployment. The evaluation uses a stratified sample of 200 questions from our published benchmark, with a gold source document for every question; the released sampling rule reads no system outputs or scores. We compare the hosted service with a governed system that resolves version and scope through explicit rules before generation. The governed system scored 97.7 overall, while the hosted service scored 88.1, a gap of 9.6 points computed from unrounded means. The question set, the answer text evaluated for both systems, the scores, and the scripts used to reproduce the reported benchmark statistics are public. The governed configuration has operated as a commercial product since January 2026 and serves 1,126 registered users; named customer organizations include Zhipu AI and Lecheng Health. By mid-April 2026, it had reached roughly 100,000 calls per workday.

</details>


### [259] [CERA-MoA: Co-Evolving Routing Mechanisms with Continually Learning LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18779)
*Jiaxuan Jiang,Liyuan He,Zhixuan Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CERA-MoA，一种路由器与智能体共同演化的迭代强化学习框架，通过预测式熟悉度估计和累积阈值自适应路由，实现MoA中路由与微调的协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mixture-of-Agents范式将查询路由与智能体微调视为独立过程，导致路由策略无法适应后训练中不断演化的智能体能力，智能体也难以实现数据驱动的协同专业化。

Method: CERA-MoA采用迭代强化学习框架，让动态路由器与独立智能体策略共同演化。设计了预测式熟悉度估计器，利用中间层隐状态评估智能体的语义胜任度，避免完整rollout开销；基于熟悉度分数，采用累积阈值自适应路由机制动态激活最小的定制化智能体子集；并根据智能体演化中的能力主动分配针对性训练样本，促进能力分化。

Result: 在多个领域的广泛实验表明，CERA-MoA优于最先进的静态智能体路由和固定工作流微调基线。

Conclusion: 路由与智能体的协同演化能有效提升MoA的任务性能与效率，并促进智能体的能力专业化分化。

Abstract: Current Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) paradigms generally treat query routing and agent fine-tuning as separate processes, limiting their ability to respond to evolving agent capabilities. This disconnect prevents routing strategies from adapting to evolving agent capabilities during post-training and prevents agents from achieving synergistic data-driven specialization. To resolve this, we introduce CERA-MoA (Co-Evolving Router with continually learning Agents for Mixture-of-Agents), an iterative reinforcement learning framework where the dynamic router and independent agent policies co-evolve. We design a predictive familiarity estimator that leverages mid-layer hidden states to evaluate semantic competence among agents, avoiding the overhead of full rollouts. Based on these familiarity scores, a cumulative-threshold adaptive routing mechanism dynamically activates a tailored minimal agent subset, achieving a trade-off between task performance and efficiency. By proactively allocating targeted training samples to agents based on their evolving competence, CERA-MoA promotes capability differentiation. Extensive experiments across various domains demonstrate that CERA-MoA outperforms state-of-the-art static-agent routing and fix-workflow fine-tuning baselines.

</details>


### [260] [Compositional Policy Violations: When Step-Level Compliance Fails In Agentic AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18820)
*Ashwini Kurady,Sri Sai Charith Grandhi,Rajesh Gupta,Sumit Mamoria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出智能体工作流的治理几乎都是"步骤级"的，而组织政策往往是"整段执行级"的，二者错配导致组合式政策违规（CPV）：每一步都合规但整体违规。作者定义CPV并提出四类分类法及对应修复原则，进而给出来源感知的运行时架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体工作流在受监管场景中做出关键决策，但现有治理手段（输入输出分类器、单轮护栏、span级评估器）都是步骤级检查。而实际政策（转介阈值、权限上限、复核要求）是整体执行的性质，步骤级谓词无法判定其不由该步骤决定的属性，因此无论步骤级监控多准都无法检出此类失败。

Method: 1) 将CPV定义为"步骤级合规无法组合"的失效，形式化为单步谓词对整体政策的不可判定性；2) 提出四类分类法：Authority Creep（权限蔓延）、Threshold Laundering（阈值洗白）、Cumulative Sum Violation（累计和违规）、Context Collapse（上下文坍缩）；3) 论证每类的正确修复由其被守护量的变异位置决定；4) 设计来源感知的运行时架构，在完整执行轨迹上评估政策，从原始溯源而非流水线派生表示重算被守护量。

Result: 给出了CPV的形式化定义与四类分类法，并建立"修复方式←被守护量变异位置"的对应关系；提出的溯源感知运行时架构可在完整轨迹上评估整段政策并重算被守护量，从而检测步骤级监控无法发现的组合违规。

Conclusion: 步骤级治理与整体政策之间存在结构性错配，导致CPV这一类无法靠提升步骤级监控精度解决的失败；必须转向执行轨迹级的政策评估，并结合原始溯源重算被守护量，按被守护量的变异位置选择对应修复策略。

Abstract: Agentic workflows now make consequential decisions in regulated settings, and the governance placed around them is almost entirely step-scoped: input-output classifiers, per turn rails, and span-level evaluators. The policies organizations actually hold, such as referral thresholds, authority limits, and review requirements, are properties of the whole execution rather than of any one step. This mismatch admits a failure mode we call a Compositional Policy Violation (CPV): every individual step passes its own check while the composed execution violates the governing policy. A predicate over a single step cannot evaluate a property that step does not determine, so no improvement in the accuracy of the step-scoped monitors detects this class. We define CPVs as the failure of step-level compliance to compose, and present a taxonomy of four types: Authority Creep, Threshold Laundering, Cumulative Sum Violation, and Context Collapse. We show that the correct repair for each class is dictated by where the guarded quantity mutates. We then introduce a provenance-aware runtime architecture that evaluates policies over complete execution traces, recomputing guarded quantities from raw provenance rather than the pipeline's derived representation.

</details>


### [261] [Infinite-Parameter LLMs: Generating and Adapting Weights from Live Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18842)
*Jinli Hu,Ross M. Clarke,Yichuan Zhang,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出无限参数LLM：用小型超网络将运行时数据转为共享基础网络的低秩调制，并在线维护贝叶斯信念，实现从实时交互中在线学习，权重有效无限而存储固定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型训练后权重冻结，无法从运行时交互数据学习，只能靠检索或提示将知识放入上下文，每次请求重读并请求结束即丢弃。作者希望架构能从实时交互中学习并把知识写入权重。

Method: 受MoE启发，用紧凑超网络把运行时数据生成共享基础网络前馈权重的低秩调制，权重由实时数据生成而非固定参数库。与以往一次读取即冻结的权重生成器不同，方法对生成器潜码维护贝叶斯信念并在线更新，使有效权重随会话进展不断重新推导。

Result: 论文提出了评估协议，用以将本方法与上下文学习和检索进行对比测试，但摘要未报告具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 将运行时知识存入权重而非提示，可在计算上摊销、释放上下文窗口、跨轮次持久，并可能比上下文使用具有更好泛化；存储占用固定但可编译权重有效无限。

Abstract: The scaling laws hold that a language model grows more capable with more parameters and more training data, and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have ridden these laws to remarkable results, activating only a fraction of an enormous stored parameter bank for each token. That success is built on static pretraining data. A deployed model faces a different world, where much of the data that would make it more useful is not in its training set but in the live interaction it is currently handling, such as the facts a user supplies or the corrections they give. A conventional model cannot learn from this data, because its weights are frozen after training. Instead, the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time are placed in the prompt, by retrieval or instruction, and re-read on every request only to be discarded once the request ends. We ask how an architecture could learn from live interaction by writing it into its weights. Taking inspiration from MoE, we propose the \textbf{Infinite-Parameter LLM}. A compact hypernetwork turns the data given at run time into a low-rank modulation of a shared base network, so the feed-forward weights are generated from live data rather than stored in a fixed bank. Where prior weight generators read the context once and freeze, we carry a Bayesian belief over the generator's latent code and update it online, so the effective weight is re-derived from that evolving belief as the session proceeds rather than fixed after one read. The stored footprint stays fixed, yet the weights the model can compile are effectively infinite. For the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time, carrying them in the weights rather than the prompt is amortized in compute, frees the context window, persists across turns, and can generalise better than in-context use. We specify an evaluation protocol that tests exactly this against in-context learning and retrieval.

</details>


### [262] [Suppressed, Not Erased: A Representational Trace of Edited Facts Survives Even Weight-Free Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18985)
*Priyansh Srivastava,Romit Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 知识编辑后隐藏状态中原始事实仍可线性解码，说明编辑是"抑制"而非"擦除"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑基准只验证局部正确性（新事实能否生成），但无法衡量原始事实在模型内部残留多少，因此需要直接研究残留知识。

Method: 提出线性痕迹探针（linear trace probe）：编辑事实后，检测原始对象是否仍能从模型隐藏状态中被线性恢复。在GPT-2-XL上测试三种机制不同的编辑器（ROME、受限微调、基于记忆的GRACE），共50个CounterFact编辑。

Result: 所有编辑生成成功率100%。原始对象仍可被线性解码，探针准确率远高于随机水平（0.50）：ROME 0.96、受限微调0.86、GRACE 0.79。GRACE不改变任何基础模型权重，却仍能解码出原始对象，说明残留痕迹并非源于不完整的权重更新。

Conclusion: 知识编辑即使行为成功，也只是在表征空间中抑制而非擦除原始关联。此外，再学习节省（relearning-savings）工具在本文设置下不可靠，作为负面方法学结果报告。

Abstract: Knowledge-editing benchmarks certify local correctness, whether an edited model produces the new fact on near-edit prompts but not how much of the original fact remains decodable inside the model. We study residual knowledge directly with a linear trace probe: after editing a fact, we ask whether the original object is still recoverable from the model's hidden states. On GPT-2-XL, across three mechanistically distinct editors applied to 50 CounterFact edits, the original object remains linearly decodable well above chance after a successful edit (probe accuracy 0.96 for ROME, 0.86 for constrained fine-tuning, and 0.79 for the memory-based editor GRACE, against a chance level of 0.50; all edits reach 100% generation-based success). The GRACE result is the most informative: GRACE changes zero base-model weights, overriding the fact through an external memory, yet the original object is still decodable from the underlying network, so the residual trace cannot be attributed to an incomplete weight update. We read this as evidence that editing, even when behaviorally successful, suppresses rather than erases the original association in representational space. We also report a relearning-savings instrument that did not behave reliably in our setting and discuss why; we treat it as a negative methodological result rather than evidence. Code and data are released.

</details>


### [263] [Function Lives Where Variance Doesn't: Task-Weighted Charts of a Language Model's Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18989)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"任务加权图"(task-weighted charts)方法，将语言模型计算维度的定义与特定功能泛函绑定。实验发现：保持困惑度需70–90%残差流宽度，主要消耗在语言稀有尾部；方差大的方向几乎不承载功能；不确定性仅需6个坐标，随深度增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "语言模型计算用多少维度"这一问题本身定义不清，除非指明所关注的泛函。作者试图将维度问题良定义化，并揭示维度随功能而异的现象。

Method: 提出 task-weighted charts 方法：在所选泛函自身的度量下，用低维坐标系拟合表示，将蒸馏转化为普通最小二乘问题。在3个家族、6个模型(70m–7B)上做实验。

Result: 保持5%以内困惑度需70–90%残差流宽度，被语言稀有尾部消耗；方差分布无预测性(GPT-2两个方向承载90%激活方差却几乎不承载功能)；模型不确定性只需6个坐标，而完整预测分布需数百个；维度随深度增加。

Conclusion: 维度是"按功能"定义的。该解离可被利用：仅保留少数维度时，按泛函度量训练的图比基于方差或最优线性压缩的方法更好地保持模型预测。

Abstract: How many dimensions does a language model's computation actually use? The question is ill-posed until one names a functional. Task-weighted charts make it well-posed: low-dimensional coordinate systems fit against a chosen functional of the representation, under the functional's own metric, turning distillation into plain least squares. Across six models from three families, spanning 70m to 7B parameters, next-token prediction needs 70--90% of the residual stream's width to stay within 5% of intact perplexity, a width consumed by the rare tail of language, and the variance profile predicts none of it: two directions carry 90% of GPT-2's activation variance and almost none of its function. Dimension is per-functional: the model's own uncertainty reads from six coordinates where the full predictive distribution needs hundreds; and it grows with depth. The dissociation is exploitable: when only a few dimensions can be kept, charts trained under the functional's metric preserve the model's predictions better than variance-based or optimal linear compression.

</details>


### [264] [Lost in Perception: Isolating Perceptual and Reasoning Failures in Multimodal Physics and Geometry Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18991)
*Raj Jaiswal,Sree Krishna Uppalapati,Dhruvkumar Patel,Ria Khatoniar,Tanuja Ganu,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过五项诊断任务，将多模态LLM的科学推理失败分解为感知、推理或两者兼有的问题，发现感知错误会显著拖累性能，且错误类型与领域相关（物理→计算错误，几何→概念误用）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态LLM虽在科学推理基准上表现强劲，但大多将感知与推理视为单一可测过程，无法定位失败究竟源于感知还是推理，因此需要一种能分离二者失败的诊断方法。

Method: 设计跨物理与几何基准的五项诊断实验，对比原始图像、人工撰写描述等不同输入形式下的准确率，并利用修正描述后的恢复情况将"感知阻塞型失败"与"真正的推理瓶颈"区分开。

Result: 错误的图示解读会降低性能，即使模型仅凭文本能正确解题；准确率从原始图像到人工描述总体上升；部分模型在修正描述下恢复率高。错误类型依领域而异：物理失败多归结为计算错误，几何失败多归结为概念误用。InternS1-mini在所有任务上均低于最弱基线，推理轨迹常提前截断。

Conclusion: 感知与推理应在多模态科学推理评估中被分开诊断；感知失败会诱发领域特异的推理错误，而科学预训练与思考能力并不必然带来鲁棒表现，评估需同时关注感知瓶颈与推理轨迹完整性。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs report strong performance on scientific reasoning benchmarks, yet most treat perception and reasoning as a single measurable process. We introduce a five-task diagnostic experiment across physics and geometry benchmarks that isolates failures to perception, reasoning, or both. Incorrect diagram interpretation degrades performance even on problems models solve correctly from text alone, and accuracy generally rises from raw images to human-authored captions. Recovery under corrected captions is high for some models, separating perception-blocked failures from genuine reasoning bottlenecks. Which reasoning error follows a perception failure depends on domain: physics failures resolve into calculation errors, geometry into conceptual misapplication. As a discussion beyond our core experiments, InternS1-mini, despite heavy scientific pretraining and thinking capabilities, falls below the weakest model from experiments on every task, with reasoning traces frequently truncating before completion.

</details>


### [265] [Compiled Agency: Frontier General-Purpose Coding Agents Build Winning Game Players from Bare Interaction - from Flappy Bird to StarCraft II and Civilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18996)
*Joey Xiao,Haonan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Gauntlet框架，让通用编程代理在无人指导下自主开发、冻结并评估游戏AI控制器，验证了"编译型代理"能力，能在完整商业游戏中获胜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理即使借助感知、记忆、技能库等脚手架，仍难将游戏知识转化为胜任的玩法。前沿编程代理的进步引出两个问题：能否真正赢得游戏，且能否在无辅助下自行构建完整玩家。

Method: 设计"开发-冻结-评估"框架Gauntlet：编程代理仅获得游戏描述、原始观察/动作接口和空策略文件，无策略/算法/架构提示。在单次自主会话中实验并工程化独立控制器，冻结后在保留实例上零模型调用地评估。

Result: 在未公开的程序化roguelike上，保留成功率0-86%，呈现明显的代际阈值（新一代系统每次会话均超过前代最佳）。在完整规模游戏中，编译后的原始API控制器击败所有公平StarCraft II内置AI及两种作弊变体；单会话程序在保留种子上通过完全征服赢得完整Freeciv游戏。

Conclusion: 这是首个无需逐回合模型调用和手工战术层、独立赢得该类型完整游戏的语言代理系统。前沿编程代理开始追踪长时程策略，冻结程序可检查，作者称之为"编译型代理"能力。

Abstract: LLM agents have repeatedly struggled to convert knowledge of a game into competent play, even when researchers build the agent around the model - supplying perception, memory, skill libraries, planners, or executable-policy scaffolds. Rapid progress in coding agents raises two sharper questions: can frontier models now win games at all, and can they win them unaided, building the entire player themselves? We introduce Gauntlet, a develop-freeze-evaluate framework that ports games from small arcades to full commercial-scale titles, behind one deliberately bare contract: a general-purpose coding agent receives a game description, a raw observation/action interface, and an empty policy file - no strategy, no algorithm, no architecture. In a single autonomous session the agent experiments with the live game and engineers a standalone controller; we freeze the result and score it on held-out instances with zero model calls during play. On an unpublished procedural roguelike, held-out success spans 0-86 percent and exposes a sharp generational threshold: every observed session of a newest-generation system outperforms the best session of its predecessor. At full-game scale, a compiled raw-API controller defeats every fair StarCraft II built-in AI and two cheating variants, and single-session programs win complete games of Civilization (Freeciv) by total conquest on held-out seeds. Though at modest rates against novice AI, this is a first: no prior language-agent system had won full games of this genre standalone, without per-turn model calls and a hand-crafted tactical layer. Frontier coding agents begin to track long-horizon strategy. The frozen programs are inspectable. We call this capability compiled agency: development experience compiled into a persistent executable agent whose architecture is built by the model.

</details>


### [266] [MUSE: Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on Multi-Modal Understanding in Situated Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19088)
*Luyao Zhu,Xun Wei Yee,Wei Li,Mun Thye Mak,Wee Siong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MUSE 基准，用于评估大视觉语言模型在情境化教育应用中对艺术图像的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多聚焦真实世界图像或特定领域教育推理，对艺术类教育内容的覆盖不足；AI 辅助语言学习需模型解读艺术图像的语义、情感与文化内容并推理视觉语境。

Method: MUSE 将图像标注与问题生成解耦，支持多样化、难度可控的任务并降低标注成本；包含十二项任务，覆盖视觉感知、语义与情感解读、文化理解及组合推理，图像侧重新加坡与东南亚多元文化语境并兼顾西方艺术传统。

Result: 对开源与闭源模型的评估显示各能力维度存在显著差距，尤其在情感解读与组合推理方面；并识别出常见失败模式。

Conclusion: MUSE 可作为标准化基准，推动情境化教育应用中的多模态理解研究，并为开发可信多模态教育模型指出关键挑战。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable progress in multi-modal understanding, yet their capabilities in educational settings remain insufficiently evaluated. In AI-assisted language learning, models must interpret artistic imagery, understand its semantic, affective, and cultural content, and reason about visual context to support meaningful interaction. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on real-world images or domain-specific educational reasoning, providing limited coverage of artistic educational content. To address this gap, we introduce MUSE, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models on artistic image understanding in situated educational applications. MUSE decouples image annotation from question generation, enabling diverse tasks with controllable difficulty while reducing annotation effort. It comprises twelve tasks spanning visual perception, semantic and affective interpretation, culture understanding, and compositional reasoning, together with diverse artistic images deliberately curated to center Singaporean and Southeast Asian multicultural contexts alongside Western art traditions, covering multiple themes and difficulty levels. Evaluation of open-source and proprietary models reveals substantial disparities across capability dimensions, particularly in affective interpretation and compositional reasoning. Our analysis further identifies common failure modes and key challenges for developing trustworthy multi-modal models for education. We hope MUSE will serve as a standardized benchmark for advancing multi-modal understanding in situated educational applications.

</details>


### [267] [Flag Game: A Toy Model for Mechanistic Swarm Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19124)
*Elizabeth Pavlova,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Flag Game 玩具模型研究 AI 智能体集体信念形成机制，揭示群体信念从崩溃到极化的转变，并提出社会电路归因与统计力学理论解释其机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体的涌现协调行为带来安全风险，而信念的快速形成与传播是关键驱动因素，需从机制层面理解以实现集体对齐。

Method: 设计 Flag Game：隐藏国旗为真值，受限智能体仅观察私有裁剪图像，但可交换信念并权衡同伴社会证据；采用社会电路归因技术预测关键智能体与观点，并通过因果干预验证；针对大规模群体发展统计力学理论。

Result: 模型重现了丰富集体现象：性能随群体规模非单调变化、社会意识提示与团队多样性提升准确率、组织结构影响显著；发现小规模信念崩溃随规模增长转为信念极化，极化导致大规模性能下降却带来信念多样性；因果干预效果随规模下降，统计力学理论与经验相图吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作迈出"机制化群体可解释性"研究第一步，揭示个体属性与通信如何产生涌现集体行为。

Abstract: Emergent coordinated behaviors of AI agents are starting to present critical safety risks. A key phenomenon driving these behaviors is the rapid formation and spread of beliefs about the world, and mechanistic understanding is crucial for collective alignment. To this end, we introduce the Flag Game, a toy model for studying the mechanisms of collective belief formation. Concretely, a hidden country flag defines the ground truth, and each bounded agent directly observes only a private crop but can exchange beliefs and weigh social evidence from peers. Despite its simplicity, the Flag Game reproduces rich collective phenomenology: non-monotonic scaling of performance with population size, accuracy gains from social-awareness prompting and team diversity, and strong effects of organizational structure. In particular, we identify that collective belief collapse at small population sizes turns into collective belief polarization as the population grows. This polarization causes the performance decline at large population sizes, but creates diversity in collective beliefs. Finally, we dissect the mechanisms underlying collective belief collapse and polarization with two complementary approaches. We first introduce social circuit attribution, a technique to predict which agent, and what view, matters most to collective dynamics, and verify its predictions by causal interventions on agents, tracing how agent patching changes collective outcomes. However, the efficacy of causal interventions on agents decreases as the population grows. We therefore develop a statistical mechanical theory for larger populations and verify that it matches the empirical phase diagram. Together, these results take a first step toward mechanistic swarm interpretability, a science of how the properties of individual agents and their communication give rise to emergent collective behavior.

</details>


### [268] [Cognitive Extensions for Dual-Process Language Agents: Memory and Self-Reflection in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19128)
*João Meneses dos Santos,Arlindo L. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在SwiftSage语言智能体上加入自适应记忆模块(AMM)与自我反思模块(SRM)，在ScienceWorld上通过四项配置消融实验，验证执行时控制是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在交互环境中表现脆弱，长时程状态跟踪、动作有效执行与失败恢复都很困难，需要识别并解决其核心瓶颈。

Method: 基于SwiftSage双过程智能体（快速动作提议器+慢速规划器），提出两个模块化认知扩展：AMM（显著性门控的情景存储与触发式检索）和SRM（有界的执行时验证与纠正干预），均以特征开关形式实现于同一执行基座，便于受控消融。

Result: 在ScienceWorld上比较baseline、+AMM、+SRM与完整系统四种配置：完整系统平均最终得分最高(64.62)，成功率最高(43.17%)，成功步效率最好(19.33步)；SRM是单独贡献最强的模块。

Conclusion: 在该设定下执行时控制是主导瓶颈；情景记忆在运行时循环被稳定之后才最有价值。

Abstract: Language agents remain brittle in interactive environments, where success requires long-horizon state tracking, valid action execution, and recovery from failed steps. We extend SwiftSage, a dual-process agent that combines a fast action proposer with a slower planner, using two modular cognitive extensions: an Adaptive Memory Module (AMM) for salience-gated episodic storage and trigger-driven retrieval, and a Self-Reflection Module (SRM) for bounded execution-time validation and corrective intervention. Both modules are implemented as feature-flagged extensions over the same execution substrate, enabling controlled ablations on ScienceWorld. Across four configurations---baseline, baseline+AMM, baseline+SRM, and the full system---the full system achieves the best mean final score (64.62), success rate (43.17%), and successful-step efficiency (19.33 steps), while SRM is the strongest standalone contributor. The results suggest that execution-time control is the dominant bottleneck in this setting, while episodic memory becomes most useful once the runtime loop is stabilized.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [269] [BLADE: ReliaBle Dynamic Hardware-Aware SNN-ANN Boundary SeLection for Event-BAseD Object DEtection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17562)
*Mahdi Taheri,Alwin Paul*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出BLADE，首个面向动态混合SNN-ANN网络的可靠性感知边界选择方法，联合优化SNN-ANN边界与ANN早退配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合SNN-ANN网络采用静态推理，仅按精度和能耗选择SNN-ANN边界，未考虑动态推理与可靠性。

Method: 联合优化SNN-ANN边界和ANN早退配置，综合考虑可靠性、检测精度、执行时间和能耗；通过层次化统计故障注入将可靠性纳入设计空间探索。

Result: 在事件相机目标检测任务上mAP 0.5达0.691，ANN早退触发时推理计算能耗降至15.82 mJ；发现浮点最高指数位是灾难性故障主因，其58.8%注入导致显著及以上精度下降，以约3%存储开销保护该位可消除灾难性故障；提高SNN计算比例可增强容错，全SNN配置在激进故障下可靠性保持度达0.965。

Conclusion: 联合优化可靠性、精度、执行时间和能耗，可支持动态混合SNN-ANN系统在安全关键边缘AI应用中的更可靠部署。

Abstract: Hybrid Spiking Neural Network (SNN)-Artificial Neural Network (ANN) architectures combine the energy efficiency of SNNs with the superior detection accuracy of ANNs for event-based object detection. Existing hybrid SNN--ANN networks, however, employ static inference and select the SNN-ANN boundary primarily according to accuracy and energy consumption, without considering dynamic inference or reliability. This paper presents BLADE, the first reliability-aware boundary selection methodology for dynamic hybrid SNN-ANN networks with ANN early exit. The proposed framework jointly optimizes the SNN-ANN boundary and ANN early-exit configuration according to reliability, detection accuracy, execution time, and energy consumption, while incorporating reliability through hierarchical statistical fault injection during design-space exploration. Experimental evaluation on an event-based object detector achieves an mAP 0.5 of 0.691 while reducing the inference compute energy to 15.82~mJ when the ANN early exit fires. Reliability analysis identifies the most significant floating-point exponent bit as the dominant source of catastrophic failures, producing significant-or-worse accuracy degradation in 58.8% of its fault injections. Protecting this single bit with approximately 3% storage overhead eliminates catastrophic failures across the evaluated realistic technology fault rates. Furthermore, increasing the proportion of SNN computation improves fault tolerance, with the fully SNN configuration achieving a reliability retention of 0.965 under aggressive fault conditions. The results demonstrate that jointly optimizing reliability, accuracy, execution time, and energy consumption enables more dependable deployment of dynamic hybrid SNN--ANN systems for safety-critical edge AI applications.

</details>


### [270] [Evolutionary Ensemble Search: Council-Guided Program Evolution with Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17590)
*Juan P. Madrigal-Cianci,Eshan Chordia*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出Evolutionary Ensemble Search (EES)，通过专家引导的程序演化构建机器学习流程，并在MLE-bench Lite上于22个任务中的19个达到奖牌阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习流程自动化搜索缺乏对执行证据、跨模态任务与累积式搜索过程的系统化整合，需要一种能持续演化的可执行搜索架构。

Method: EES由角色专业化委员会生成结构化搜索方向，编排器将其分配给执行专家与演化引擎；引擎选择父代、诊断错误，通过代码突变、结构化流程编辑与交叉产生子代，每个子代须执行并获得验证证据；种群档案保留替代方案，兼容预测经验证门控集成，并通过父代相对算子孙、会话记忆与问题索引经验实现自适应。

Result: MLE-bench Lite开发账本显示19/22任务（86.36%）达到奖牌阈值，最佳成绩为11金、5银、3铜，覆盖文本、图像、表格、音频、科学几何与确定性变换。

Conclusion: 该结果为带等级反馈与外部来源的公开开发成果，非盲测自主智能体成功率；贡献在于可累积可执行搜索的具体架构及其跨模态开发结果的版本化记录。

Abstract: Evolutionary Ensemble Search (EES) constructs machine-learning procedures through expert-guided program evolution. A role-specialized council turns task evidence and experimental results into structured search directions. An orchestrator allocates these directions to execution specialists and an evolutionary engine. The engine selects measured parents, diagnoses their errors, and produces descendants through code mutation, structured pipeline edits, and crossover. Each child must execute and acquire its own validation evidence. Population archives retain useful alternatives, while compatible predictions compete in a validation-gated ensemble stage. Search adapts through parent-relative operator credit, session memory, and problem-indexed lessons retrieved across runs. We specify these mechanisms, distinguish their execution profiles, and define the contracts required to compare candidates as their computations change. A public MLE-bench Lite development ledger records medal-threshold artifacts on 19 of 22 tasks (86.36\%), with best outcomes of 11 gold, five silver, and three bronze. The procedures span text, images, tables, audio, scientific geometry, and deterministic transformations. The campaign includes grade feedback between runs, external-source routes, and mixed confirmation procedures; its aggregate is an achieved development result, not a blind autonomous-agent success rate. The report contributes a concrete architecture for cumulative executable search and a versioned account of its cross-modal development outcomes.

</details>


### [271] [The Cost of Becoming: Developmental Encoding Increases Phenotypic Diversity but Reduces Locality and Recombination Robustness in Evolved Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17606)
*Lyes Saad Saoud*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控的进化机器人基准，比较直接编码、静态生成编码和时间型合子发育编码三种表征，揭示发育编码在扩大表型可达范围的同时，削弱了已适应结构的局部遗传，形成一种表征层面的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发育编码常被认为能通过结构化的基因型-表型过程提升可进化性、鲁棒性与适应性，但扩大表型变异的编码也可能使有用的亲本结构在突变和重组下更难保留。作者旨在系统检验这一权衡。

Method: 采用受控进化机器人基准，对比三种匹配表征：直接编码、静态生成编码、时间型合子发育编码。三组使用34个基因组参数、34个成年控制器参数、相同初始基因组矩阵、相同优化器、相同突变与交叉算子、相同环境和相同适应度评估次数，每种表征进行30次配对进化运行。

Result: 发育编码在最终训练适应度和分布外适应度上并未稳定超过直接编码，但显著提升了最终种群表型多样性和突变可达表型多样性。同时表现出更低的突变存活率、更低的基因型-表型局部性、交叉后代适应度与存活率骤降、重组新颖性增加；持续发育动态还能衰减发育早期施加的损伤。

Conclusion: 发育编码存在表征层面的权衡：能扩大可达表型邻域并提供发育内损伤衰减，却损害已适应结构的局部继承。因此评价发育编码不应只看性能，而应综合考虑生成性、局部性、鲁棒性和继承兼容性的联合几何。

Abstract: Developmental encodings are often motivated by the expectation that a structured genotype-to-phenotype process can improve evolvability, robustness, and adaptation. Yet an encoding that expands phenotypic variation may simultaneously make useful parental structure harder to preserve under mutation and recombination. We test this trade-off in a controlled evolutionary-robotics benchmark comparing three matched representations: direct encoding, a static generative encoding, and a temporal zygotic developmental encoding. All three conditions use 34 genome parameters, 34 adult-controller parameters, identical initial genome matrices, the same optimizer, the same mutation and crossover operators, the same environments, and the same number of fitness evaluations. Across 30 paired evolutionary runs per representation, developmental encoding does not reliably exceed direct encoding in final training or out-of-distribution fitness. It does, however, substantially increase final population phenotypic diversity and mutant-reachable phenotype diversity. The same representation exhibits lower mutation viability, lower genotype--phenotype locality, sharply reduced crossover offspring fitness and viability, and increased recombination novelty. Continued developmental dynamics also attenuate damage applied early in development relative to otherwise identical late damage. These results identify a representation-level trade-off: development can enlarge the neighborhood of reachable phenotypes and provide within-development damage attenuation while degrading the local inheritance of already-adapted structure. We argue that developmental encodings should therefore be evaluated not by performance alone, but by a joint geometry of generativity, locality, robustness, and inheritance compatibility.

</details>


### [272] [Benchmarking Tabular Foundation Models as Surrogates in Expensive Evolutionary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18130)
*Lu Han,Jin Wang,Yuchen Li,Haoran Gu,Shulei Liu,Ziyang Shi,Wenao Lu,Handing Wang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文通过大量实验与理论分析，系统研究TabPFN作为代理模型在昂贵优化问题中的有效性，发现其效果高度依赖具体问题，不能普遍替代传统代理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理辅助进化算法（SAEAs）依赖代理模型求解昂贵优化问题，而TabPFN因强大预测能力被引入作为代理模型并展现出潜力，但其在SAEAs中的有效性尚缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 结合广泛实验与深入理论分析，在离线与在线SAEA设置下，覆盖单目标、多目标、约束、组合、混合变量及工程优化等多种问题场景进行评测，并分析TabPFN的优势与局限。

Result: TabPFN的有效性高度依赖具体问题，无法普遍替代传统代理模型；其适用性取决于数据可用性、适应度景观复杂度、搜索空间特征及在算法中的角色。

Conclusion: 应根据具体优化场景有选择地采用TabPFN，并通过定制化模型管理策略和角色特定的算法设计来充分发挥其优势、规避其缺陷。同时为不同优化设置下的应用提供了实践指南。

Abstract: Surrogate-assisted evolutionary algorithms (SAEAs) are effective methods for solving expensive optimization problems (EOPs), where surrogate models replace most expensive evaluations and critically influence the final optimization results. In recent years, tabular foundation models have advanced rapidly, and the Tabular Prior-data Fitted Network (TabPFN) has been adopted as a surrogate model for EOPs due to its strong predictive capability, demonstrating promising performance. Motivated by its potential as a surrogate model in SAEAs, this work conducts a comprehensive study that combines extensive experiments with in-depth theoretical analysis to investigate the effectiveness of TabPFN. Specifically, we perform experiments across both offline and online SAEA settings, covering diverse problem scenarios such as single-objective, multi-objective, constrained, combinatorial, mixed-variable, and engineering optimization problems. In addition, we further analyze the advantages and limitations of TabPFN within SAEAs and provide practical guidelines for its application in different optimization settings. Results show that the effectiveness of TabPFN is highly problem dependent, and it cannot replace conventional surrogates universally. Overall, TabPFN should be adopted selectively according to data availability, landscape complexity, search space characteristics, and its role within the algorithm. Customized model management strategies and role-specific algorithm design are necessary to fully exploit its advantages and avoid its pitfalls.

</details>


### [273] [A Metaheuristic Optimization Framework for Discrete Optimization under Strict Time Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18702)
*Umut Çalıkyılmaz,Nitin Nayak,Sven Groppe*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出STILO——一个专为严格时间限制下优化设计的元启发式优化框架，集成ACO、GA和SA的细粒度配置空间，实验表明算法与算子的有效性同时取决于问题类型、实例特征和可用计算预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时应用需要在毫秒级内求解复杂优化问题，元启发式优化框架（MOF）虽能提供通用搜索机制，但现有工作大多忽略了将可用计算时间作为显式分析维度。

Method: 提出STILO框架，融合蚁群优化（ACO）、遗传算法（GA）和模拟退火（SA）的细粒度配置空间，结合现有与新颖算子；并针对SA提出离散距离计算机制、针对ACO提出与问题无关的图结构；在合成与基准实例上进行多类离散优化问题实验。

Result: 所提SA离散距离计算机制在严格时间限制下有效；ACO的问题无关图结构相对有效性会随时间限制变化，即使实例特征相同；算法族与算子的有效性不仅依赖问题类型，还取决于实例特征与可用计算预算。

Conclusion: 优化方法的性能评估应将计算时间预算作为显式维度，STILO为严格时间限制下的元启发式优化提供了有效工具，并揭示了算法/算子有效性对时间预算的敏感性。

Abstract: Real-time applications often rely on optimization approaches that can find high-quality solutions to hard problems on the order of milliseconds. Metaheuristic optimization frameworks (MOFs) are useful tools for such tasks, as they provide large sets of general-purpose search mechanisms that can return solutions under different computational budgets. However, existing work largely overlooks the available computation time as an explicit dimension of analysis. In this work, we introduce STILO, a MOF specifically designed for optimization under strict time limits. STILO integrates fine-grained configuration spaces for ant colony optimization (ACO), genetic algorithm (GA), and simulated annealing (SA), combining existing and novel operators. We performed experiments using both synthetic and benchmark instances of various discrete optimization problems. The results indicate that the proposed discrete distance calculation mechanism for SA is useful for optimization under strict time limits. They also show that the relative effectiveness of the proposed problem-independent graph structures for ACO can vary across time limits, even for the same instance characteristics. More generally, the results demonstrate that the effectiveness of algorithm families and operators depends not only on the problem type, but also on the characteristics of the instance and the available computational budget.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [274] [Scaling Articulated Rationales for MLLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17639)
*Haoke Xiao,Yueyang Liu,Yuhui Zhang,Xiang Chen,Yufei Liu,Jia Xu,Yalong Guan,Xiaolan Zhu,Xiaoyu Zhang,Shijun Wang,Shuang Yang,Zijie Meng,Zejian Zhang,Ruochen Yang,Xiangyu Wu,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Lantao Hu,Cheng Luo,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出SARA框架，将稀疏的用户自然语言偏好解释(AUR)转化为可扩展的工业推荐信号，通过数据引擎、MLLM对齐与排序模型三阶段实现，并在快手线上部署验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统主要依赖点击、观看时长等隐式行为推断偏好，但这些信号只反映用户"做了什么"，而非"为何喜欢或不喜欢"。用户显式表达的偏好解释(AUR)具有极性和原因层面的信息价值，但因其稀疏、低质、覆盖率低而难以在工业系统使用。

Method: SARA包含三部分：(1)数据引擎，从2.4亿快手直播用户中采集并整理AUR，构建质量可控、以作者为中心的SARA-HQ数据集；(2)将通用MLLM通过大规模SFT和Quality-Refining DPO对齐为SARA-7B，把理由生成从8.6万覆盖作者扩展到全部1000万作者空间；(3)SARA-Ranker通过理由感知交互建模和拒绝记忆建模，将生成的正负理由整合进生产排序。

Result: 离线评估、人工校准和线上A/B测试表明，SARA-7B生成的理据比强MLLM基线更具体、极性更一致、更接地气；SARA-Ranker提升参与度并减少负面反馈。系统已每日刷新部署超过30天。

Conclusion: SARA确立了显式理由作为工业推荐系统中实用的一等文本信号，验证了将稀疏AUR转化为可扩展推荐信号的可行性。

Abstract: Modern recommendation systems largely infer user preferences from implicit behaviors such as clicks, watch time, and negative feedback, but these signals reveal what users do rather than why they like or dislike content. This work studies articulated user rationales (AURs), i.e., users' natural-language explanations of their preferences, as a new class of polarity-aware and reason-level textual signals for recommendation. Despite their potential value, AURs are difficult to use in industrial systems because they are naturally sparse, often low-quality, and only cover a small fraction of items. We present SARA (Scaling Articulated Rationales), an industrial framework that turns sparse AURs into scalable recommendation signals. SARA first builds a data engine that elicits and curates AURs from 240M Kuaishou Live users, producing SARA-HQ, a quality-controlled and author-centric rationale dataset. It then aligns a general-purpose MLLM into SARA-7B through large-scale SFT and Quality-Refining DPO, extending rationale generation from 86,564 AUR-covered authors to the full 10M-author space. Finally, SARA-Ranker integrates the generated positive and negative rationales into production ranking via rationale-aware interaction modeling and rejection-memory modeling. Extensive offline evaluation, human calibration, and online A/B tests show that SARA-7B generates more specific, polarity-consistent, and grounded rationales than strong MLLM baselines, while SARA-Ranker improves engagement and reduces negative feedback in production. Deployed with daily refresh for over 30 days, SARA establishes articulated rationales as a practical, first-class textual signal for industrial recommendation systems.

</details>


### [275] [One Size Does Not Fit All! Dynamic Retriever and Generator Selection for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17709)
*Neeraj Anand,Payel Santra,Partha Basuchowdhuri,Debasis Ganguly,Sumit Bhatia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了检索器与生成器复杂度在RAG中的交互效应，发现更强检索通常比更强生成带来更大收益但均存在递减与非单调回报，据此提出查询自适应的DRAG框架（含免训练的DRAG_QPP和有监督的DRAG_SFT），在保持或提升效果的同时显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统对查询采用固定的检索器与生成器配置，忽略查询复杂度差异，导致计算资源分配低效；检索与生成的适应性虽被分别研究，但其联合影响缺乏系统探索。

Method: 系统分析检索器与生成器复杂度在factoid与multi-hop QA（含bridge、composition）上的交互作用；提出DRAG查询自适应框架：DRAG_QPP用Query Performance Prediction信号选检索器、用检索上下文的困惑度选生成器（免训练路由）；DRAG_SFT微调LLM联合预测检索器-生成器配置（监督路由）。

Result: 更强检索通常比增加生成努力收益更大，但两者均呈递减且非单调回报，高复杂度配置并非普遍更优；在三个LLM家族与四个QA基准上，DRAG_QPP性能可与强静态基线相当并大幅降低延迟，DRAG_SFT则持续优于静态与免训练自适应基线。

Conclusion: 联合自适应检索与生成能比静态RAG获得更优的效果-效率权衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems typically employ fixed retriever and generator configurations across queries, despite substantial differences in query complexity and information needs, leading to inefficient allocation of computational resources. While retrieval and generation adaptivity have been studied independently, their joint effect on end-to-end RAG performance remains underexplored. We systematically analyze how retriever and generator complexity interacts across factoid and multi-hop question answering (QA), including bridge and composition reasoning tasks. Our analysis shows that stronger retrieval generally yields larger gains than increased generation effort, but both exhibit diminishing and non-monotonic returns, indicating that higher-complexity configurations are not uniformly better across queries. Motivated by these findings, we introduce DRAG, a query-adaptive framework for selecting retriever-generator configurations. We first propose DRAG$_\text{QPP}$, a training-free routing approach that uses Query Performance Prediction (QPP) signals to guide retriever selection and perplexity-based measures over retrieved context to guide generator selection. We further introduce DRAG$_\text{SFT}$, a supervised routing approach that fine-tunes an LLM to jointly predict retriever-generator configurations. Across three LLM families and four QA benchmarks, \qpprag~achieves performance comparable to strong static RAG baselines while substantially reducing inference latency, whereas DRAG$_\text{SFT}$ consistently improves effectiveness over static and training-free adaptive baselines. Overall, DRAG demonstrates that jointly adapting retrieval and generation achieves a more favorable effectiveness-efficiency trade-off than static RAG pipelines.

</details>


### [276] [DUPAR: Dual-Path Conversational Retrieval via Speech Retriever with Cross-Turn Evidence Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18042)
*Yuanjun Li,Yiwen Liu,Dapeng Li,Zhiwei Xu,Bin Zhang,Shengtao Zhang,Rong Shen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 DUPAR，一种快慢双路径的对话语音检索框架，缓解 ASR 级联延迟与跨模态不对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 ASR→文本检索级联会引入延迟并传播识别错误，而直接语音检索又面临跨模态对齐困难，因此需要兼顾效率与准确性的方案。

Method: 快路径用任务适配的音频编码器对齐冻结的 BGE-M3 文本嵌入，检索跨轮证据缓存；缓存置信度不足时，慢路径融合音频与 ASR 转写嵌入做全索引检索，并通过一跳图扩展刷新下一轮证据缓存。

Result: 在领域知识库上，干净语音下音频编码器接近文本检索精度，查询侧比 ASR+文本编码器快 3.75×；噪声基准上平均 Recall@10 从 0.771 提升至 0.875，合成说话风格下整体 Recall@1 提升 4.2 个百分点；跨轮缓存显著降低一跳邻块跟随检索的错误。

Conclusion: DUPAR 通过快慢路径与跨轮证据缓存，在效率与鲁棒性间取得平衡，有效提升对话语音检索性能。

Abstract: Voice assistants grounded in external knowledge typically use automatic speech recognition (ASR) to transcribe speech queries before retrieving evidence from textual knowledge bases. This cascade adds latency and propagates recognition errors, whereas direct speech retrieval is vulnerable to cross-modal misalignment. To address these limitations, we propose DUPAR, a conversational retrieval framework with complementary slow and fast paths. The fast path uses a task-adapted audio encoder aligned with frozen BGE-M3 text embeddings to search a cross-turn evidence cache. When cache confidence is insufficient, the slow path fuses full-index retrieval using audio and ASR-transcript embeddings, and the selected evidence refreshes the next-turn evidence cache through one-hop graph expansion. On a domain-specific knowledge base, our trained audio encoder approaches text-retrieval accuracy on clean speech with a 3.75$\times$ query-side speedup over ASR + Text Encoder. It raises average Recall@10 from 0.771 to 0.875 on the noise benchmark and improves overall Recall@1 by 4.2 percentage points across synthesized speaking styles. Compared with full-index audio retrieval, cross-turn evidence caching significantly reduces retrieval errors when the previous turn retrieves correct evidence and the follow-up targets a one-hop neighboring chunk.

</details>


### [277] [PageRecall: Measuring Page Selection in Literature-Grounded Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18154)
*Aaditya Chauhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文揭示文献问答中证据定位的瓶颈在于检索而非阅读，主张放弃页面选择、整体展示论文，并以解析方式处理位置型问题，最终取得0.762论文F1、0.441证据F1、0.920选择题准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LitTraceQA要求从27,487篇论文中检索、定位证据页面与图表并按指定格式作答。作者发现管线的主要瓶颈是检索：金标页面只有52.6%被送到证据定位模型面前。

Method: 核心改动是"停止选择"：既然每篇检索到的论文都能放进上下文，就直接整篇展示；页面排序仅作为论文过长时的兜底。同时把参考文献解析为可寻址列表，以应对"第24篇参考文献的第一作者"这类位置型问题。

Result: 给定页面时，定位模型48次中45次引用正确页（94%）；页面缺失时45例中14次返回空、24次给错页、仅7次给对页，静默失败约为显式失败的两倍。最终测试集得分：论文F1 0.762，证据F1 0.441，选择题准确率0.920；可解析论文上金标页召回率达100%。

Conclusion: 证据接地受限于检索而非阅读能力，整篇展示是有效修正。由于管线依赖无种子控制的闭源模型，作者发布验证工具以对照提交产物核验论文核心论断。

Abstract: We describe our system for LitTraceQA (GroundLM @ EMNLP 2026): given a research question, retrieve the relevant papers from a pool of 27,487, cite the page and the table or figure where the answer lives, and answer in a requested format. Our main finding is that evidence grounding is limited by retrieval, not by reading. The page selector put the annotator's page, which we call the gold page, in front of the model that locates evidence only about half the time (52.6% gold-page recall), while that model, given the page, cited the right one in 45 of the 48 locators it emitted (94%). When the page was missing it rarely said so: of 45 such cases it returned nothing 14 times, a wrong page 24 times, and a correct page 7 times, so the pipeline failed quietly almost twice as often as it failed visibly. Since the failure was that the right page was never shown, the fix is to stop choosing: each retrieved paper fits in the model's context, so we show it whole. Page ranking survives only as a fallback inside papers too long to fit, which no test-split paper was, and gold-page recall reaches 100% on the papers we can parse. Separately, questions that identify their target by position rather than content, such as "the first author of the 24th reference", are served by parsing rather than retrieval: we resolve the bibliography into an addressable list, which also supplies identifiers the evidence metric scores. The final system scores 0.762 paper $F_1$, 0.441 evidence $F_1$ and 0.920 multiple-choice accuracy on the held-out test split. Because the pipeline depends on a closed model without seed control, we release a harness that verifies the paper's central claims against committed artifacts.

</details>


### [278] [Single-Token Expected-Value Scoring for Cold-Start Candidate Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18188)
*Qihang Wang,Jinwei Tan,Mengyuan Shi,Mayank Sharma,Shuai Zhao,Fuxian Li,Ryan Yan,Alexander P. Kreuzer,Mohit Jain,Dheeraj Toshniwal,Manoj Seethamsetty*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出单token期望值打分排序方法，将候选-职位相关性建模为对{1,...,5}等级token的有序分类，用小型语言模型微调，在求职者与雇主相关性两个维度上均优于基线和零样本LLM，并已在线验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助招聘可减轻人工筛选负担，但生产级排序器部署困难：零样本LLM打分不稳定、非确定且排序不准；传统深度排序模型需要数百万次交互日志，而低流量小众招聘平台无法提供。平台只有几十万条有序相关性标签，规模虽小但足够，因为预训练语言模型已编码任务所需的通用世界知识。

Method: 提出单token期望值打分：把候选-职位相关性视为对等级token{1,...,5}的有序分类，用首token概率分布的期望作为相关性分数。由于只做一次解码，分数是模型logits的确定性函数，无需解析输出，延迟低。为从监督中学习异质招聘标准的非线性依赖，用混合有序回归损失微调小型语言模型，结合保持有序距离的MSE项与锐化类别边界的交叉熵项。

Result: 沿求职者相关性和雇主相关性两个维度，用NDCG@10和低相关率评估。离线：微调模型优于启发式基线和零样本LLM。端到端模拟中效果方向一致且幅度更大（求职者NDCG@10 +54.2%，低相关率 -46.7%）。线上实验降低雇主低相关率27.3%，提高雇主保留率7.07%。

Conclusion: 单token期望值打分结合混合有序回归损失，为低流量招聘平台提供了确定、低延迟且有效的排序方案，离线、模拟与在线实验一致验证了其优越性。

Abstract: AI-assisted sourcing streamlines candidate review, reducing the administrative burden of manual screening for recruiters. However, deploying language models as production rankers remains challenging. Zero-shot Large Language Models (LLMs) may produce unstable, non-deterministic scores and rank less accurately, while conventional deep neural rankers require millions of logged interactions that a low-traffic, niche sourcing platform does not produce. What is available instead is a few hundred thousand ordinal relevance labels -- small by ranker-training standards, but sufficient when a pretrained language model already encodes the general world knowledge the task depends on.
  We present single-token expected-value scoring, a ranking primitive that casts candidate-job relevance as an ordinal classification over the grade tokens {1, ..., 5} and reads the relevance score as the expectation of the first-token probability distribution. Because the score comes from a single decoding step rather than open-ended generation, it is a deterministic function of the model's logits, requires no output parsing, and serves at low latency. To learn the non-linear interdependencies of heterogeneous hiring criteria from this supervision alone, we fine-tune a Small Language Model (SLM) with a hybrid ordinal regression loss combining a Mean Squared Error term, which preserves ordinal distance, with a categorical Cross-Entropy term, which sharpens class boundaries.
  We evaluate along two dimensions -- Jobseeker Relevance and Employer Relevance -- using NDCG@10 and low relevance rate. Offline, our fine-tuned model outperforms a heuristic baseline and zero-shot LLMs. An end-to-end simulation shows the same direction at larger magnitude (+54.2% Jobseeker NDCG@10, -46.7% low relevance rate), and a live online experiment reduces employer low-relevance by 27.3% and raises employer keep rate by 7.07%.

</details>


### [279] [Quanta: A Self-Contained Python Library for Hybrid Retrieval over Quantised Embeddings, Lexical Indexes, and Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18248)
*Ioannis E. Livieris*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Quanta 是一个开源 Python 库，将 4-bit 量化稠密向量检索、BM25 全文检索和图遍历统一到单一检索 API 下，并采用加权倒数排名融合与"图作候选扩展器"两项设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 流程通常由多个独立系统（ANN 索引、全文搜索引擎、图数据库、关系文档存储）拼装而成，各自有部署、配置和故障模式，集成逻辑还需重复开发，缺乏统一的混合检索方案。

Method: Quanta 统一三类检索信号于单一 API，并作出两点设计承诺：(1) 用加权倒数排名融合（weighted RRF）组合信号，而非将异构分数归一化到统一范围（后者是查询依赖的、well-posed 性存疑）；(2) 图仅作为候选扩展器而非相关性打分器，遍历扩大候选池，新文档在标识符白名单下由稠密索引重新打分。

Result: 论文为设计理念与库架构介绍，摘要中未给出定量实验结果。

Conclusion: Quanta 通过统一 API、加权 RRF 融合与"图作候选扩展、内容决定排序"的分离设计，区别于现有混合检索栈，简化了多系统集成并明确了结构邻接与内容证据的各自角色。

Abstract: An advanced retrieval-augmented generation pipeline is typically assembled from three or four independently operated systems: an approximate nearest-neighbour index, a full-text search engine, a graph database, and a relational document store. Each contributes its own deployment surface, configuration model, and failure modes, and the integration logic that binds them is written anew in every project. In this work, we present \textsc{Quanta}, an open-source Python library, which unifies dense vector search over 4-bit quantised embeddings, BM25 full-text retrieval, and knowledge-graph traversal behind a single retrieval API. Quanta makes two design commitments, which distinguish it from existing hybrid retrieval stacks. First, signals are combined by \emph{weighted reciprocal rank fusion} rather than by normalising heterogeneous scores onto a shared range, which we argue is ill-posed because such normalisations are query-dependent. Second, the graph is a \emph{candidate expander and not a relevance scorer}: traversal widens the candidate pool, and the newly admitted documents are re-scored by the dense indexes under an identifier allowlist, so structural adjacency determines what is considered while content evidence determines how it ranks.

</details>


### [280] [One-Step Retrieval Framework for Real-Time Sponsored Search Ads Using Hierarchical Text Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18296)
*Tongtong Liu,Renyu Zhang,Jiayu Ding,Hongchao Guo,Xintao Yang,He Wei,Zhaoyu Li,Haiyang Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ANGLE框架，用LLM生成层次化文本表示并统一检索、相关性与排序于单一LLM，在真实搜索广告场景中提升消费1.81%、GMV 2.16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多阶段级联检索各模块独立优化，目标不一致且会过早淘汰高潜候选；现有LLM生成式方法依赖离散SID，需大量SID-广告映射记忆，泛化差、维护成本高、解码低效，且依赖小奖励模型评估商业价值。

Method: ANGLE采用LLM生成的层次化文本表示（商业意图提供高层概览、广告摘要提供细粒度细节），并在单一LLM中集成检索、相关性与排序，充分利用LLM能力实现精准高效排序。

Result: 真实搜索场景中消费提升1.81%，GMV提升2.16%；离线评测对比七个基线，在HR、ACR等关键指标上全面领先。

Conclusion: ANGLE以统一生成-判别-排序框架克服传统级联与SID方法的局限，兼顾效果与效率，验证了单LLM统一建模在实时广告检索中的有效性。

Abstract: Traditional retrieval systems typically use multi-stage cascading architectures (MCA), where each module is optimized independently, leading to inconsistent objectives and the premature elimination of high-potential candidates. Recent LLM-based generation methods offer end-to-end solutions but use discrete semantic identifiers (SIDs) to retrieve ads, which are not learned by the base LLM and require memorization of numerous SID-to-ad mappings during SFT, suffering from limited generalization to unseen ads, high maintenance and update costs. The one-to-one mapping between SIDs and advertisements leads to inefficient decoding. Moreover, these methods rely on a small reward model (e.g. pctr) for relevance and ranking, limiting the LLM's ability to fully assess ads' commercial value. To address these challenges, we propose A uNified Generation-discriminative-ranking reaL-time rEtrieval (ANGLE) framework. ANGLE uses LLM-generated hierarchical textual representations, which consist of commercial intent that provide high-level overviews and ad abstract that deliver fine-grained details. Additionally, ANGLE integrates retrieval, relevance, and ranking directly within a single LLM, enabling precise and efficient ranking of ads by leveraging the full capabilities of the LLM. We applied ANGLE to the real-world search scenarios, achieving a 1.81% increase in consumption and a 2.16% increase in gross merchandise volume (GMV). We also conducted offline evaluations of ANGLE and seven baselines, with ANGLE outperforming all across key metrics such as HR and ACR.

</details>


### [281] [Exploring LLMs and RAG for Plausible and Explainable Material Prediction of Vehicle Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18437)
*Frederik Wagner,Annerose Eichel,Sabine Schulte im Walde*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究探索LLM能否在无需大量微调的情况下，准确预测和解释车辆零部件（如刹车盘、喷油器）的候选材料。对比了生成式LLM基线、单次RAG和迭代CoVe三种方法，使用经过领域过滤的维基百科语料。结果显示LLM生成显著优于先前工作，而RAG方法未能进一步提升，并指出RAG系统面临的超参数优化、高质量合法语料获取及专家评估设计等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在车辆零部件材料预测与解释任务上的能力，验证是否无需大量微调即可实现，并评估检索增强方法能否带来增益。

Method: 测试三种方法：标准生成式LLM基线、单次RAG、迭代Chain-of-Verification（CoVe）变体；检索基于公开的领域过滤维基百科语料；因无金标准，开发了支持结构化领域专家评估的定制网页标注工具。

Result: LLM生成显著优于先前工作；测试的RAG方法未能进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: RAG系统仍面临挑战：超参数优化、高质量且合法可访问的领域语料稀缺，以及专家评估研究设计问题。

Abstract: In this work, we explore whether LLMs can accurately predict and explain plausible materials for vehicle components such as brake discs or fuel injectors without requiring extensive fine-tuning. We test and evaluate three approaches: a standard generative LLM baseline, a single-pass Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) approach, and an iterative Chain-of-Verification (CoVe) variant. For retrieval, we rely on publicly available data using a domain-filtered Wikipedia corpus. Since no gold standard exists for this task, we develop a custom web-based annotation tool supporting crucial functions for structured domain expert evaluation. LLM-based generation substantially outperforms prior work, which is not further surpassed by the tested RAG approaches. Our results surface remaining challenges for RAG-based systems: hyperparameter optimization, the availability of high-quality, legally accessible domain corpora, and expert evaluation study design.

</details>


### [282] [SURF: Subtractive Updates for Recommender Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18695)
*Filippo Betello,Antonio Purificato,Nicola Tonellotto,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SURF是一个用于序列推荐系统的轻量级近似机器遗忘框架，通过三阶段操作实现高效遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户隐私需求和GDPR等法规使机器遗忘成为现代推荐系统的必要要求。但序列推荐系统因其依赖时间交互模式而面临独特挑战，现有方法要么需要计算成本高昂的完全重训练，要么未能考虑用户行为的序列性。

Method: SURF分三阶段：(i)在嵌入空间中识别待遗忘物品的邻域；(ii)在该紧凑局部子集上训练辅助模型；(iii)推理时将辅助模型分数从原模型分数中减去。

Result: 在7个数据集上与5个基线对比，SURF的遗忘效果可与完全重训练相当，同时大幅降低计算成本：NDCG@20最高提升32%，仅需原重训练基线时间预算的2%。

Conclusion: SURF为序列推荐系统提供了一种高效、低成本的近似机器遗忘方案，兼顾遗忘有效性与计算效率。

Abstract: The increasing demand for user privacy and compliance with regulations such as GDPR has made machine unlearning a fundamental requirement for modern recommender systems. However, Sequential Recommender Systems (SRS) pose unique challenges for unlearning due to their reliance on temporal interaction patterns. Existing approaches either require computationally prohibitive full retraining or fail to account for the sequential nature of user behavior. We propose SURF (Subtractive Updates for Recommender Forgetting), a lightweight framework for approximate machine unlearning in SRS. SURF operates in three stages: (i) identifying the neighborhood of the item to forget in the embedding space, (ii) training an auxiliary model on this compact local subset, and (iii) subtracting the auxiliary model's scores from the original model at inference time. Experiments against five baselines on 7 datasets show that SURF achieves unlearning effectiveness comparable to full retraining while substantially reducing computational cost, yielding up to a 32% improvement in NDCG@20 while requiring just 2% of the original retraining baseline time budget. We share our code at https://github.com/FilippoBetello/SURF.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [283] [Not All Agents Are Equal: Code Quality and Post-Merge Maintenance Across Five Autonomous Coding Agents in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17598)
*Obada Kraishan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模实证研究商业AI编码代理提交的PR在合并后的质量与维护表现，发现质量差异具有厂商特异性，代理代码安全气味反而更少，但审查负担分布不均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码代理如今能以两年前难以企及的规模在公共仓库提交PR，但人们对这些代码"落地"后的实际表现（安全性、可维护性、后续改动等）知之甚少，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: 结合AIDev数据集与58,792条缓存的GitHub API响应，分析2024年12月至2025年7月间2,807个仓库中来自五家商业代理（OpenAI Codex、Devin、GitHub Copilot、Cursor、Claude Code）的37,623个带来源标注的PR，并配以匹配的人类基线，测量新增代码的安全气味、结构可维护性、合并后churn、回滚率及人工审查行为。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 质量差异因厂商而异——Codex的PR回滚率约为人类的一半（6.1% vs 11.5%，OR=0.50），而Devin回滚率更高（14.5%，OR=1.31）；(2) 汇总各厂商的代理代码比人类代码更少出现安全气味（OR=0.63），主要源于硬编码凭证和eval类结构更少；(3) 审查负担分布不均——Copilot的PR获得最多人工审查与变更请求，Claude Code的PR首次人工审查等待最久（中位数12.6小时）。

Conclusion: AI编码代理的代码质量并非统一优于或劣于人类，而是呈现厂商特异性差异；代理代码在安全气味上反而略占优势，但给人工审查带来的负担分布不均。所有流水线代码、统计报告与图表均已公开以供复现。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents now open pull requests in public repositories at a scale that was out of reach two years ago, yet little is known about what happens to that code after it lands. This paper studies 37,623 provenance-labeled pull requests (PRs) from five commercial agents (OpenAI Codex, Devin, GitHub Copilot, Cursor, and Claude Code) and a matched human baseline, drawn from 2,807 GitHub repositories between December 2024 and July 2025. We combine the AIDev dataset with 58,792 cached GitHub API responses to measure security smells in added code, structural maintainability, post-merge churn, revert rates, and human review behavior. Three results stand out. First, quality differences are vendor-specific rather than uniform: Codex-authored PRs were reverted about half as often as human PRs (6.1% vs. 11.5%, odds ratio 0.50), while Devin PRs were reverted more often (14.5%, odds ratio 1.31). Second, agent code pooled across vendors was less likely than human code to contain a security smell (odds ratio 0.63), driven by fewer hardcoded credentials and eval-style constructs. Third, review effort concentrates unevenly: Copilot PRs drew the most human reviews and change requests, and Claude Code PRs waited the longest for a first human review (median 12.6 hours). All pipeline code, statistical reports, and figures are released for replication.

</details>


### [284] [Quiescence in Concert: Composing Multi-Channel Time-Outs for IOCO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17640)
*Laura Brandán Briones,Petra van den Bos,Marcus Gerhold*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出多通道提升算子，使各组件可在各自通道上携带独立超时，从而在保持MBT一致性与测试生成的同时简化实时系统测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时系统测试理论丰富但实践应用不足，部分原因是应用时间机制需要实践者不具备的专业知识。此前工作用典范提升算子解决了带时间测试问题。

Method: 引入多通道提升，允许每个组件在自己的通道上携带自己的超时；证明提升与并行组合可交换（先组合再提升等于先提升再组合）。

Result: 证明了提升与并行组合交换；MBT框架在基于超时测试器可观察的可测试迹上得以保留，包括一致性、测试生成和判定结果。

Conclusion: 多通道提升在保持MBT测试能力的同时，进一步降低了实时系统测试中对时间自动机专业知识的要求。

Abstract: In Model-Based Testing (MBT), test suites are generated automatically from a formal specification. The theory of testing real-time systems is rich, but often underused in practice, partly because applying the timed machinery demands expertise practitioners should not need. In prior work we addressed this for timed testing with a canonic lifting operator, which lets a modeller specify behaviour as plain labelled transition systems while implicitly obtaining the timed automata that express their quiescent behaviour (the explicit absence of outputs) with timers. This paper takes the next step: we show that our lifting still works when each component carries its own time-out on its own channel. This way, we introduce a multi-channel lifting. We show that it commutes with parallel composition, i.e. composing and lifting is the same as lifting first and then composing. We show that the MBT apparatus survives: conformance, test generation and verdicts are preserved by the lifting, on the testable traces that a time-out based tester can observe.

</details>


### [285] [CARE: A Responsibility-Oriented Architecture for Domain-Scoped Resolution in Modular and Upgradeable Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17647)
*Ali Rajabi Nekoo,Laleh Rasoul,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE——一种面向责任的模块化智能合约架构，通过显式责任边界与领域范围的能力解析，将执行协调与目标选择授权分离，并用形式化模型、TLA+验证和实证评估加以论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化与可升级合约虽支持替换执行逻辑，但模块化分派本身无法界定谁拥有协调、解析、共享状态、可复用服务与观测等架构责任，导致责任边界模糊、演进困难。

Method: 提出CARE责任导向架构，引入领域范围的能力解析，分离执行协调与选择执行目标的授权；建立形式化架构模型推导五项结构属性；用TLA+编码行为语义并通过TLC针对七项架构不变量验证（中间结果用于迭代精化模型）；在此基础上与EIP-2535及仅路由基线进行受控实证对比，并开展对抗性测试。

Result: 在评估负载中，CARE带来约固定7.7k gas的运行时路由开销；局部能力替换成本接近仅路由对照组，且显著低于所评估的EIP-2535重构路径。对抗性测试刻画了授权控制及委托执行与重入相关的残余风险。

Conclusion: CARE应定位为一种实现显式责任归属与局部演进的架构模型，而非对现有智能合约可升级性机制的通用替代方案。

Abstract: Modular and upgradeable smart-contract systems enable replaceable execution logic, but modular dispatch alone does not define which components own architectural responsibilities such as coordination, resolution, shared state, reusable services, and observation. This paper presents CARE, a responsibility-oriented architecture that makes these ownership boundaries explicit and introduces domain-scoped capability resolution, separating execution coordination from the authority that selects an execution target. CARE is defined through a formal architectural model from which five structural properties are derived. Its behavioral semantics are further encoded in TLA+ and evaluated with TLC against seven architectural invariants. Intermediate TLC results were used to refine the architectural model before the final validation run. A controlled empirical evaluation against EIP-2535 and a routing only baseline shows that CARE introduces an approximately fixed 7.7k-gas runtime routing premium in the evaluated workloads, while localized capability replacement remains close to the routing-only control and substantially less expensive than the evaluated EIP-2535 reconfiguration path. Adversarial tests further characterize authorization controls and residual risks associated with delegated execution and reentrancy. These results position CARE as an architectural model for explicit responsibility ownership and localized evolution rather than as a universal replacement for existing smart-contract upgradeability mechanisms

</details>


### [286] [A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Quality Assurance Practices and Gaps in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17698)
*Wuyang Dai,Moses Openja,Jiho Shin,Hung Viet Pham,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对157个开源LLM智能体项目的大规模实证研究，揭示其QA实践在功能测试、防护措施和端到端验证方面存在明显不足与碎片化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体整合规划、记忆、工具调用、代码执行和外部交互，自主性增强的同时带来新的可靠性、安全与安保风险，然而其质量保障（QA）实践现状尚缺乏系统性了解。

Method: 选取157个GitHub星标≥100的开源LLM智能体项目，分析其文档、源代码、配置与测试，刻画执行面、防护措施、测试工件、风险场景及反复出现的缺口。

Result: 当前QA主要聚焦基础功能与高风险动作，覆盖碎片化；等效执行路径间防护措施应用不一致；测试很少考察边界、对抗或多步工具使用失败；识别出的风险很少转化为端到端QA检查。

Conclusion: 需超越特性级测试，转向系统化端到端验证，确保智能体工作流在面对不可信输入、工具、持久状态和外部API时仍处于预期边界内。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used across software engineering, web automation, research, and productivity applications. Their integration of planning, memory, tool use, code execution, and external interactions enables greater autonomy but also introduces new reliability, safety, and security risks. We present a large-scale empirical study of quality assurance (QA) practices in 157 open-source LLM-based agent projects with at least 100 GitHub stars. We analyze documentation, source code, configurations, and tests to characterize QA practices across execution surfaces, safeguards, testing artifacts, risk scenarios, and recurring gaps. We find that current QA primarily focuses on basic functionality and high-risk actions, while coverage remains fragmented. Safeguards are inconsistently applied across equivalent execution routes, tests rarely examine boundary, adversarial, or multi-step tool-use failures, and identified risks are seldom translated into end-to-end QA checks. These findings highlight the need to move beyond feature-level testing toward systematic end-to-end validation that ensures agent workflows remain within intended boundaries when interacting with untrusted inputs, tools, persistent state, and external APIs.

</details>


### [287] [Detecting Argument-Swap Bugs Using Context-Enhanced Code Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17844)
*Subrata Das,Ali Aman,Muhammad Asaduzzaman,Kawser Wazed Nafi,Salimur Choudhury*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出BugProbe，一种无需调用-定义映射的学习型方法，检测Python方法调用中参数顺序错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码元素名称蕴含语义信息，可用于缺陷检测等任务。已有方法依赖参数名与方法形参名的词法相似度及调用-定义映射，但在Python等动态类型语言中此类映射难以获得。

Method: BugProbe结合本地上下文与参数使用上下文等多源信息，将基于名称的相似度与机器学习结合，构建方法参数的表征，无需调用-定义映射。数据集来自top-1000 starred GitHub仓库的132,739个Python源文件，生成3,371,244个合成训练样本。

Result: 在标准评价指标上取得高准确率，并持续优于当前最优基线。同时贡献了由提交历史人工验证的55个真实参数交换缺陷基准。

Conclusion: 无需显式调用-定义解析即可有效检测参数顺序缺陷，适合动态类型语言场景。

Abstract: Names of source code elements convey rich semantic information and have been widely used in software engineering tasks such as bug detection, code completion, type prediction, and code classification. Prior studies exploit lexical similarity between method arguments and formal parameter names to detect bugs caused by incorrectly ordered arguments, typically relying on establishing mappings between method calls and their corresponding definitions. However, such mappings are often difficult to obtain in dynamically typed languages like Python. In this paper, we present BugProbe, a learning-based approach for detecting incorrectly ordered arguments in Python method calls that does not require call-to-definition mappings. Our approach leverages multiple sources of contextual information, including local context and argument usage context, and combines name-based similarity with machine learning to construct expressive representations of method arguments. We collect a new dataset of 132,739 Python source files from the top-1,000 starred GitHub repositories, yielding 3,371,244 synthetic training examples, and contribute a curated benchmark of 55 real-world argument-swap bugs manually verified from commit histories. We evaluate our approach on this dataset and show that it achieves high accuracy and consistently outperforms a state-of-the-art baseline across standard evaluation metrics. These results demonstrate that effective detection of argument-ordering bugs is possible without relying on explicit call-to-definition resolution, making the approach well suited for dynamically typed language settings.

</details>


### [288] [Experimental Settings in LLM-Based Program Repair: A Study of Inputs, Tool Access, Feedback, and Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17993)
*Xushu Dai,Yicheng Cai,Nanqing Luo,Pei-Yu Tseng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个明确规范APR实验结果实验设置的框架与机器可读schema，揭示仅凭benchmark无法重建repair task，改进可复现性并明确跨系统比较范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APR评测通常只报告benchmark、修复缺陷数和验证测试，但这些信息已无法完整描述呈现给系统的repair任务。LLM-based系统在repair前信息、允许的仓库/测试操作、失败后反馈上差异巨大，使同一benchmark可实例化为从局部补丁生成到仓库级诊断与迭代修复的不同任务。

Method: 提出一个显式规范APR结果实验设置的框架；分析Defects4J和SWE-bench上报告的实验设置，用task unit、fault-localization assumptions、initial input、tool access、repair-time feedback、final validation、resource budget刻画每个结果；并引入机器可读schema。

Result: 分析表明benchmark identity本身不足以重建被评测任务，也不足以确定跨报告修复率比较的合适范围。

Conclusion: 引入机器可读schema来规范每个实验设置，以提高可复现性并使跨系统比较的范围明确。

Abstract: Evaluations of automated program repair (APR) systems commonly report the benchmark, the number of repaired defects, and the tests used for final patch validation, but these items no longer fully specify the repair task presented to a system. Recent LLM-based systems differ in the information supplied before repair, the repository and testing operations permitted during repair, and the feedback returned after unsuccessful attempts, allowing the same benchmark to instantiate substantially different repair tasks ranging from localized patch generation to repository-level diagnosis and iterative repair. We present a framework for explicitly specifying the experimental settings associated with reported APR results. We analyze reported experimental settings from systems evaluated on Defects4J and SWE-bench and characterize each result by its task unit, fault-localization assumptions, initial input, tool access, repair-time feedback, final validation, and resource budget. Our analysis shows that benchmark identity alone is insufficient to reconstruct the evaluated task or determine the appropriate scope of comparison across reported repair rates. We therefore introduce a machine-readable schema for specifying each experimental setting to improve reproducibility and make the scope of cross-system comparisons explicit.

</details>


### [289] [An Empirical Evaluation of Cost-Efficient Large Language Models on Algorithmic Programming Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18052)
*Chandimal Adikari,Nandika Herath*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究评估低成本LLM生成企业级Java代码的可靠性：结构合规接近完美，但仅12.9%答案正确，38.4%方法并未真正计算返回值，可靠性与正确性呈反相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业需要判断低成本LLM能否可信地按规格生成生产代码，而现有评估多关注结构合规而非真实计算与答案正确性。

Method: 用3个模型（Gemini Flash 3、GPT-5.4 mini、Claude Haiku 4.5），4种模型×工具组合×2种提示词共8种配置，解决992道算法题并写成符合指定签名和DTO规范的Spring Boot方法；禁止迭代与硬编码答案，预留8题探测缺失输入；对7,593个方法按8类结果分类并部署执行，得到7,936次请求测量。

Result: 结构合规接近上限，但38.4%方法未计算其返回值，仅12.9%返回答案正确；响应可靠性与正确性反相关，真正计算的方法反而最少作答，正确率19.3%。

Conclusion: 低成本LLM生成的代码虽形式上合规，但计算真实性和答案正确性严重不足，不能仅凭结构合规判定其可信；研究受单次生成、覆盖不全、语法分类及语料污染等限制。

Abstract: This study empirically evaluates whether cost-efficient Large Language Models (LLMs) can be trusted to generate enterprise code to a written specification. Three models (Gemini Flash 3, GPT-5.4 mini and Claude Haiku 4.5) were asked to solve 992 algorithmic problems as Java Spring Boot service methods conforming to a mandated signature and data-transfer-object specification, crossing four model and agentic coding tool combinations with two prompt variants to yield eight configurations, with iteration forbidden and hardcoded answers explicitly prohibited. Eight problem statements were withheld to probe how models respond to missing input. The 7,593 resulting methods were classified by an eight-class outcome taxonomy describing what each does about producing an answer, then deployed and executed, giving 7,936 measured requests joined to that classification. Structural conformance approached ceiling, yet 38.4% of methods do not compute the value they returned and only 12.9% of returned answers were correct. Conditioning on outcome class shows that response reliability and correctness are inversely related, whereas genuinely computing methods answered least often and were correct 19.3%. Limitations include single generation runs per configuration, partial harness coverage, single-pass timing, syntactic classification, and probable corpus contamination.

</details>


### [290] [Relationally Guided Use Case Modeling with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18291)
*Guangyu Wang,Bangqi Li,Ji Wu,Zhijun Shao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FlowGen 通过 LLM 语义信息处理（SIP）、语义关系图（SRG）与增强 R-GAT，实现完整用例流程构建，包括基本流生成（BFGen）、分支点预测（BPP）和分支条件替代流生成（AFGen），在三项核心任务上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用例流程手动构建成本高且依赖专家经验；现有自动化方法难以保持语义一致性、控制流逻辑、数据流逻辑及系统边界，尤其在识别分支点和生成替代流方面表现不足。

Method: FlowGen 使用基于 LLM 的 SIP 提取语义元素，构建由增强 R-GAT 编码的 SRG 用于 BFGen；并通过 BPP 预测分支点，通过 AFGen 生成分支条件驱动的替代流。

Result: 在 13 个公开和 7 个工业数据集上，FlowGen 三个核心组件均优于竞争基线：BFGen 的 Precision 提升 14%，Recall 提升 7-25%，F1 提升 11-30%，AUC 提升 10-19%；BPP 的 Precision 提升 30-110%，Recall 提升 33-91%，F1 提升 32-117%；AFGen 的 Precision 提升 8-23%，F1 提升 5-18%，AUC 提升 0.6-2.5%。

Conclusion: FlowGen 能有效支持完整用例流程构建并显著优于现有方法；同时验证了 LLM-based SIP 模块和 BFGen 中注意力保留因子的有效性，并分析了需求完整性对 BFGen 的影响及分支上下文范围对 AFGen 的影响。

Abstract: Use case flows are important elements of use case modeling because they support downstream software engineering activities, including requirements analysis, architectural and detailed design, and test case generation. However, constructing them manually is costly and expertise-intensive, while existing automated approaches still struggle to preserve semantic consistency, control-flow logic, data-flow logic, and the intended system boundary, especially when identifying branch points and generating alternative flows. To address this problem, we propose FlowGen for complete use case flow construction. FlowGen uses LLM-based Semantic Information Processing (SIP) to extract semantic elements, constructs a Semantic Relational Graph (SRG) encoded by an enhanced R-GAT for basic flow generation (BFGen), and further supports branch point prediction through BPP and branch-conditioned alternative flow generation through AFGen. Evaluations on 13 public and 7 industrial datasets show that FlowGen consistently outperforms competitive baselines in all three core components. In particular, BFGen improves over the best baseline by 14% in Precision, 7-25% in Recall, 11-30% in F1, and 10-19% in AUC; BPP improves Precision by 30-110%, Recall by 33-91%, and F1 by 32-117%; AFGen improves Precision by 8-23%, F1 by 5-18%, and AUC by 0.6-2.5%. Moreover, we validate the effectiveness of the LLM-based SIP module and the attention preservation factor in BFGen, analyze the impact of requirement completeness on BFGen, and examine how different scopes of branch-related context affect AFGen.

</details>


### [291] [A Study of the Reliability of Agentic AI-Generated Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18298)
*Ayesha Shafique,Barton P. MIller,Elisa R. Heymann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过将Agentic-AI工作流生成的十个Linux工具程序与人工编写的发布级版本对比，使用模糊测试（黑盒与AFL++灰盒）评估可靠性，发现AI生成代码通常与人工版本一样可靠甚至更可靠，但更容易出现挂起（如死循环），更少出现内存错误，且高度依赖提示词与人监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic-AI软件开发宣称能更快完成软件、提高程序员效率并生成更可靠代码，但如何客观验证这些说法尚不明确，因此需要一个基于真实基准的可靠评估方法。

Method: 采用典型最佳实践的Agentic-AI开发工作流；目标程序为十个知名、发布级的人工编写Linux工具程序作为基准真值；使用模糊随机测试（经典黑盒生成式测试与AFL++覆盖引导的灰盒变异测试）衡量可靠性，并进行对比分析。

Result: AI生成的工具程序通常与最新人工版本一样可靠，甚至更可靠；AI版本的失败更少见，但失败类型不同于人工版本——更少内存错误（如缓冲区溢出），更多挂起（如死循环）。同时验证了生成稳健可靠软件需要认真实践和人工监督，代码质量高度依赖提示词、技能及人类引导者的应对方式。

Conclusion: Agentic-AI可用于开发可靠软件，但其质量取决于提示词与人工监督；采用包含提示词和技能的Agentic-AI工作流可作为代码规格说明，实现软件的低成本可持续维护。

Abstract: Agentic-AI based software development offers the promise of faster completion of the software, greater programmer efficiency, and more reliable code. The question is how can we verify these claims in an objective way? In this project, we attempted to answer this question based on three practices.
