First, we applied a typical best-practices agentic AI workflow for software development. Second, our target programs were ten well-known, release-quality human-written Linux utility programs so that we could compare the AI-generated code against a concrete ground truth. Third, we based our measure of reliability on a widely used testing technique, fuzz random testing. For this testing, we used both classic black box, generational testing and more modern coverage guided (gray box, mutational) testing using AFL++. We found that the AI-generated versions of the utility programs were typically as reliable - often more reliable - than the latest human-generated versions of these programs. While the AI-generated versions did have some failures, they were less common than the code from the standard repositories. Interestingly, the AI-generated code was less likely to have failures such as memory errors (such as buffer overflows) but more likely to have hangs such as infinite loops. In addition, we verified that generating robust and reliable software using agentic AI requires careful practice and human supervision. The quality of the code is highly dependent on the prompts and skills used, and how the human directing the process responds. We also demonstrated that using agentic AI workflow for software development (with its prompts and skills) can become a specification of the code that leads to cost-effective sustainability of the software.

</details>


### [292] [A Study on the Impact of Natural Language Differences in Prompts on Automatic Code Generation Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18311)
*Haruka Tokumasu,Masanari Kondo,Alexander Serebrenik,Dong Wang,Kei Koyanagi,Kotaro Noguchi,Naoyasu Ubayashi,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究自然语言（英日中）对LLM代码生成准确率的影响（语言偏差），并评估翻译作为缓解策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代码生成研究众多，但输入提示中自然语言对性能的影响（语言偏差）尚未被探索。

Method: 在AtCoder、LeetCode、BigCodeBench上将同一问题以英/日/中三种语言呈现，测试7个LLM（GPT-4o、o3-mini、DeepSeek-V3.2、Llama-3、Qwen2.5-Coder-14B/0.5B、GitHub Copilot），以Accuracy（全部测试用例通过的问题数）衡量，比较翻译前后的准确率。

Result: 问题陈述语言影响代码生成性能；各数据集官方支持语言达到最高中位准确率；翻译可提升准确率但效果因数据集和模型类型而异；AtCoder含较多叙述式及更长的题目。

Conclusion: 自然语言显著影响代码生成准确率；翻译可部分缓解语言偏差，效果取决于数据集与模型；输入的叙述性与上下文长度是关键因素。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance in automatic code generation tasks, thereby encouraging new research in this area. Although numerous studies have explored LLM-based code generation, the impact of the natural language in input prompts remains unexplored (language bias). This study aims to (1) quantify how the natural language of input prompts influences LLM-based code generation performance and (2) evaluate a mitigation strategy to reduce language bias in code generation. We assess code generation Accuracy on AtCoder, LeetCode, and BigCodeBench. To quantify the language bias on code generation, each problem is presented in English, Japanese, and Chinese. We use seven LLMs (GPT-4o, o3-mini, DeepSeek-V3.2, Llama-3, Qwen2.5-Coder-14B, Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B, and GitHub Copilot) and assess their performance in terms of Accuracy (the number of problems for which generated code passes all test cases). We compare Accuracy before and after translation to evaluate the effectiveness of translation as a mitigation strategy. We observed that the natural language of problem statements affects LLM-based code generation performance. Specifically, the languages officially supported by each dataset achieved the highest median Accuracy. Also, translation improved Accuracy, but its effectiveness was not consistent across datasets and model types. We found that AtCoder contained a particularly high proportion of narrative-style problem statements and longer problem statements. Natural language significantly affects LLM code generation accuracy. Translation can mitigate language bias in some settings, but its effectiveness depends on the dataset and model type. Furthermore, the narrative aspects and context length of input prompts are important factors related to language bias and the effectiveness of translation as a mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [293] [From Component Snapshots to Lifecycle Traces: Agent-Based Software Composition Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18391)
*Chaofan Li,Zhengduo Xue,Chengxiang Li,Yutao Hu,Yueming Wu,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出SCA-Agent，基于智能体的生命周期感知软件成分分析，跨Code、Build、Release、Deploy、Runtime五阶段重建组件溯源轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SCA方法仅能对某个生命周期阶段做快照分析，无法刻画依赖在开发到执行过程中被解析、移除、重打包、转换后的跨阶段关系，导致组件来源与最终归宿难以追踪。

Method: SCA-Agent自适应探索项目特定分析路径，采集各阶段证据，并跨阶段关联观测，恢复组件身份、版本、引入路径、传播关系与最终生命周期状态。

Result: 在Java、JavaScript、Python生态105个真实项目上评估。各阶段和各生态组件检测F1最高；漏洞暴露评估F1达96.69%，比最佳传统SCA工具高18.76个百分点。

Conclusion: 生命周期感知SCA可实现可追溯的组件来源分析，并支持更准确的软件供应链风险评估。

Abstract: Software supply-chain security requires accurate identification of third-party components and an understanding of how they evolve from development to execution. Existing software composition analysis (SCA) approaches examine manifests, build environments, release artifacts, containers, or runtime states, but typically produce only stage-specific views of software composition. As dependencies are resolved, removed, repackaged, and transformed across lifecycle stages, a single snapshot cannot capture both where a component originates and where it ultimately ends up. Combining snapshots from multiple stages still leaves their cross-stage relationships unresolved. We present SCA-Agent, an agent-based approach to lifecycle-aware SCA that reconstructs evidence-backed component lifecycle traces across Code, Build, Release, Deploy, and Runtime. SCA-Agent adaptively explores project-specific analysis paths, gathers stage-specific evidence, and correlates observations across stages to recover component identities, versions, introduction paths, propagation relationships, and final lifecycle states. We evaluate SCA-Agent on 105 real-world projects from the Java, JavaScript, and Python ecosystems. SCA-Agent achieves the highest component detection F1 across all lifecycle stages and ecosystems. For vulnerability exposure assessment, it reaches an F1 score of 96.69%, exceeding the best traditional SCA tool by 18.76 percentage points. These results show that lifecycle-aware SCA supports traceable component provenance and more accurate software supply-chain risk assessment.

</details>


### [294] [Evaluating the Health of Open-Source Smart City Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18801)
*Rodrigo Bravo Simões,Fernando Brito e Abreu,Vasco Amaral*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估多个开源智慧城市平台（SCP）的"OSS健康度"，以帮助资源有限的市政IT团队做出采用决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智慧城市平台作为IoT设备与智慧服务之间的中间件，可简化城市管理。开源SCP免费且利于数字主权、避免厂商锁定，对预算有限的市政具有吸引力。但缺乏技术支持会阻碍其被IT团队采用，因此需要评估其生态健康度。

Method: 运用"OSS健康"（OSS health）概念，从开发者与用户协作、安装与用户手册、无漏洞且有文档的代码、测试套件等多方面特征，对不同的开源SCP进行评估。

Result: 对多个开源智慧城市平台进行了基于OSS健康度各维度特征的对比评估。

Conclusion: OSS健康度是市政采用开源SCP的重要决策因素，评估结果可辅助资源有限的市政IT团队选择合适的平台。

Abstract: To manage the complexity of smart cities, a variety of smart city platforms (SCPs), both proprietary and open-source, have been proposed. Typically acting as middleware between IoT devices at a lower layer and smart services at a higher layer, these platforms simplify the management of smart cities by reifying a variety of requirements common to different municipalities. Open-source software (OSS) is typically free of charge, making open-source SCPs an attractive option for municipalities with smaller budgets that still wish to improve efficiency and quality of life for residents and visitors. Furthermore, using an OSS SCP promotes digital sovereignty, since municipalities can control where the data will reside and will be less prone to vendor lock-in.
  A lack of support for OSS hinders its adoption by municipalities' IT teams, often with scarce technical resources. An active (aka "healthy") ecosystem, including well-orchestrated developers and users, the availability of installation and user manuals, non-vulnerable and documented code, test batteries, and other artifacts, is an important decision factor before adopting an OSS SCP. In this paper, we evaluate different open-source SCPs using the concept of "OSS health", covering several of the aforementioned characteristics.

</details>


### [295] [ProgramDistill: From Interactive Web Apps to Verifiable Reference-Guided SWE Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18805)
*Jeonghye Kim,Minseon Kim,Young Jin Kim,Matheus Pereira,Marc-Alexandre Côté,Alessandro Sordoni,Xingdi Yuan,Zhengyan Shi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ProgramDistill 基准，通过分解全功能参考应用为可重放的行为特征，评估编码智能体从交互式软件中推断并实现功能的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体评估多依赖 issue 或指令指定的期望行为，但实际 Web 开发中智能体需从可运行软件中推断行为并在不完整应用中实现，缺乏此类评估基准。

Method: 构建 mine-craft-patch 流水线，将应用分解为不同粒度的特征，每个特征关联可通过 gold patch 执行的可重放行为；无人工干预生成 1,975 个可重放验证行为、覆盖 26 个应用、4,063 个任务。

Result: 在九个前沿编码智能体上，GPT-6 Astra 与 Claude Opus 5 在全应用重建的累积工作流中成功率为 49.2% 和 28.8%；部分应用重建中，恢复深度从 1 增至 8 时成功率从 100% 降至 64.0%、从 96% 降至 32%。

Conclusion: ProgramDistill 提供了可扩展、难度可控的基准，用于评估和诊断编码智能体，并为未来课程式训练提供自然基础。

Abstract: Coding agents are typically evaluated with desired behavior specified through issues or instructions. In practical web development, however, agents may need to infer behavior from working software and implement it in an incomplete application. We introduce ProgramDistill, a benchmark evaluating coding agents on features discovered through interaction with fully functional reference applications. We build ProgramDistill by factorizing applications into features of different granularities, each associated with replayable behaviors executable via its gold patch. Our pipeline, mine-craft-patch, discovers 1,975 replay-verified behaviors across 26 applications and constructs 4,063 tasks without human intervention. Across nine frontier coding agents, GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5 achieve 49.2% and 28.8% success on cumulative workflows in full-application reconstruction. In partial-application reconstruction, success falls from 100% to 64.0% and from 96% to 32% as restoration depth increases from 1 to 8. ProgramDistill thus provides a scalable benchmark with controlled difficulty for evaluating and diagnosing coding agents, and a natural basis for future curriculum-based training.

</details>


### [296] [Removing Noise or Introducing Bias? The Hidden Cost of MSR Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18953)
*Mohit Kaushik,Jyoti Bawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析MSR研究中常用的过滤标准对样本人口特征带来的偏差，揭示其隐藏成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub等OSS平台是MSR研究的主要数据源，但用户群体广泛，并非所有仓库都是真实工程项目，研究者常采用各种标准过滤噪声，这可能从根本上改变样本人口特征。

Method: 分析SEART平台上157万个仓库，基于MSR研究中常用阈值构建多个数据集，考察过滤过程带来的偏差。

Result: 发现过滤过程存在维护偏差，掩盖了OSS项目真实的废弃率（73.42%）；过滤策略偏向某些生态系统和治理风格，并扭曲变量间关系，产生关系偏差。

Conclusion: 研究者应采用分层抽样并细化噪声检测标准，以避免上述偏差。

Abstract: OSS platforms like GitHub serve as a primary data source for MSR research. As the platform is widely used by different users, spanning from student to developer, not all repositories are actual engineered projects. Therefore, to avoid such noise, researchers often apply several criteria, which may fundamentally change the sample demographics. To understand such biases, this study aims to uncover the hidden cost originating from these arbitrary thresholds or criteria. We analyzed 1.57 million repositories from the SEART platform and constructed several datasets from the thresholds often applied in MSR research. We identify the maintenance bias in these filtering processes, which masks the true abandonment (73.42%) realities of OSS projects. Also, these strategies favor some ecosystems and governance styles. Moreover, the sampling strategy also distorts the relationship between variables, suffering from relational biases. Therefore, to avoid such biases, the researchers should shift towards stratified sampling and refining the criteria for noise detection.

</details>


### [297] [Code Consistency Preference Optimization Verification for Language Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19002)
*Yunlong Tan,Mingqiao Mo,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出执行一致性偏好优化方法，通过依赖图与执行一致性分数生成配对数据微调模型，在数学与物理推理任务上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Bradley-Terry奖励模型的偏好优化难以捕捉科学任务所需的逻辑依赖与执行一致性，而基于执行的验证虽有计算可靠性优势，却未被偏好优化有效利用。

Method: 基于UltraFeedback构建科学推理数据集；提取推理步骤表达式、前置条件与可推导关系，构建依赖图并计算执行一致性分数；将分数附加到每步生成配对训练数据，用于微调Llama-3-8B与DeepSeekMath-7B；并扩展科学可行性控制框架。

Result: MATH提升+17.0%，GSM8K提升+15.1%；PhyX多模态物理推理达50.1%，超过DeepSeek-R1(49.8%)与OpenAI o3-mini(48.2%)，alpha=0.10时科学有效性覆盖达91.7%，科学定律违反减少73%。

Conclusion: 依赖图与执行一致性的偏好优化能有效提升LLM科学推理能力与科学有效性，形成CCPO模型系列。

Abstract: Execution-based verification enhances large language models' mathematical reasoning through computational soundness and dependency-aware filtering. However, prior preference optimization methods relying on Bradley-Terry reward models fail to capture the logical dependencies and execution consistency needed for scientific tasks. We propose a method that generates computationally sound solutions with dependency graphs for execution-consistent preference optimization. We first build a scientific reasoning dataset using UltraFeedback prompts, model generations, verification, and consistency results. Then we extract reasoning step expressions, prerequisites, and derivability relationships to construct dependency graphs and compute execution consistency scores. These scores are appended to each step, creating paired training data. Fine-tuning Llama-3-8B and DeepSeekMath-7B yields significant gains: +17.0% on MATH and +15.1% on GSM8K. Extending our Scientific Feasibility Control framework achieves 50.1% accuracy on PhyX multimodal physics reasoning, surpassing DeepSeek-R1 (49.8%) and OpenAI o3-mini (48.2%), with 91.7% scientific validity coverage at alpha=0.10 and 73% fewer scientific law violations, resulting in the CCPO model family.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [298] [Computing Stationary Equilibria in Measure-Dependent Markov Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17584)
*Jing Dong,Bar Light,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个计算框架，用于求解反馈通过有限维聚合变量作用于转移规律的"测度依赖马尔可夫系统"的平稳均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运营与经济中的许多随机系统存在长期状态分布与转移规律之间的反馈，需高效计算此类平稳均衡。

Method: 将平稳均衡问题归约为有限维自洽方程，分离稳态分析与均衡计算；依据自洽映射性质选择不动点迭代、松弛迭代或残差最小化，并用有限时间无穷小扰动分析估计所需导数。

Result: 给出导数估计器及误差界，区分蒙特卡洛误差与有限时间偏差；通过战略型G/G/c排队与观点动力学模型展示不同结构性质对应不同计算方法。

Conclusion: 该框架将均衡计算与稳态分析解耦，并为选择合适算法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Many stochastic systems in operations and economics exhibit feedback between their long-run state distribution and the transition law governing their dynamics. In this paper, we develop a computational framework for stationary equilibria in such measure-dependent Markov systems when this feedback operates through a finite-dimensional aggregate. We show that the original stationary-equilibrium problem can be reduced to a finite-dimensional self-consistency equation, separating steady-state analysis of the underlying Markov system from equilibrium computation. We use properties of the resulting self-consistency map to guide the choice among fixed-point iteration, relaxed fixed-point iteration, and minimization of the fixed-point residual. The last approach requires derivatives of the self-consistency map, which are typically unavailable in closed form. We therefore develop finite-time infinitesimal perturbation analysis estimators for these derivatives, with error bounds that separate Monte Carlo error from finite-time bias. We illustrate the framework through a strategic $G/G/c$ queue and an opinion-dynamics model, showing how different structural properties lead naturally to different equilibrium-computation methods.

</details>


### [299] [Decentralized Optimal Equilibrium Learning Over Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17601)
*Seref Taha Kiremitci,Muhammed O. Sayin*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了动态通信网络上的有限正规形式博弈中去中心化学习社会最优均衡的问题，提出基于语义信号的去中心化学习动力学，并给出有限时间对数遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态通信网络中，每个智能体只能观测自身收益、事先不知道博弈结构，且仅能低带宽地与随时间变化的邻居通信，因此需要一种完全去中心化且适应动态拓扑的均衡学习方法。

Method: 提出网络化去中心化最优均衡学习动力学：智能体基于局部收益比较生成随机化的语义“满意/不满意”信号，并交换带时间戳、时间堆叠的表格而非原始动作、收益信息或本地估计/参数；结合表格融合与时间多数重建以缓解动态通信影响，并在同相探索扰动下进行。

Result: 在功利主义和比例公平社会福利目标下，建立了最优均衡选择的有限时间对数遗憾保证；仿真结果进一步表明该方法能在动态通信网络上有效选择社会合意均衡。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，仅依靠低带宽语义信号与时间堆叠表格交换，即可在动态通信网络中实现去中心化的社会最优均衡学习，并具备理论上的有限时间性能保证。

Abstract: This paper studies decentralized learning of socially optimal equilibria in finite normal-form games over dynamic communication networks. Each agent observes only its own realized payoffs, does not know the game a priori, and can communicate only with time-varying neighbors using low-bandwidth messages. We propose networked decentralized optimal equilibrium learning dynamics in which agents generate randomized semantic content/discontent signals from local payoff comparisons and exchange time-stamped time-stacked tables rather than raw actions, payoff information or local estimates/parameters. The method combines table fusion with temporal majority reconstruction to mitigate dynamic communication while preserving fully decentralized operation. We establish finite-time logarithmic regret guarantees, with an in-phase exploration perturbation, for optimal equilibrium selection under utilitarian and proportional-fair social welfare objectives. Simulation results further show that the proposed approach can effectively select socially desirable equilibria over dynamic communication networks.

</details>


### [300] [Learning Market Competition in Shared Spectrum: A Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17754)
*Qixuan Zai,Randall Berry*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究在需求函数未知的共享频谱市场中，竞争性无线服务提供商使用多智能体强化学习（MARL）学习定价/数量策略，并考察是否出现默契合谋及其与竞争模式和MARL算法选择的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有共享频谱市场竞争模型假设需求函数已知，无法刻画现实中SP需通过交互逐步学习市场需求的场景，也未探讨学习型决策系统可能引发的默契合谋问题。

Method: 将需求函数未知的共享频谱市场竞争建模为多智能体强化学习（MARL）过程，让竞争的SP在学习市场动态的同时调整竞争策略，并比较伯特兰（价格）与古诺（数量）两种竞争模式及不同MARL算法。

Result: 实验表明共享频谱市场中会出现类似默契合谋的行为，且其普遍程度依赖于竞争模式（价格 vs 数量）与所选的MARL算法。

Conclusion: 揭示了学习动态、市场结构与频谱共享之间的相互作用，对无线市场设计及学习型决策系统的部署具有启示意义。

Abstract: This paper investigates market competition among wireless service providers (SPs) that serve customers using shared spectrum. Prior work has analyzed such markets through models of competition with congestible resources, capturing both the congestion-sensitive nature of wireless spectrum and the effects of spectrum sharing on service quality. These models typically assume that the market demand function is known, enabling SPs to optimize pricing or quantity decisions under either Bertrand or Cournot competition. In contrast, we consider a setting in which the demand function is initially unknown and must be learned over time. We model this learning process using multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), allowing competing SPs to learn market dynamics while adapting their competitive strategies. Although MARL has shown strong performance in a variety of economic settings, recent work has demonstrated that it can also give rise to tacit collusion among self-interested agents. We therefore examine whether similar collusive behavior emerges in shared-spectrum markets and how its prevalence depends on the mode of competition (price versus quantity) and the choice of MARL algorithm. Our results provide insight into the interaction between learning dynamics, market structure, and spectrum sharing, with implications for both wireless market design and the deployment of learning-enabled decision-making systems.

</details>


### [301] [Estimate then Predict: Convex Formulation for Travel Demand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17919)
*Youngseo Kim,Gioele Zardini,Samitha Samaranayake,Soroosh Shafiee*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 提出一种凸规划方法，在分层扩展logit框架内统一目的地、方式和路径选择，兼顾行为刻画与估计框架，并具备全局最优性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四步法易产生分布、方式划分与交通分配间的不一致；既有组合模型受限于简化行为假设、计算负担及缺乏统一参数估计框架。

Method: 构建凸规划模型，整合目的地、方式与路径选择；采用分层扩展logit，通过嵌套logit刻画方式相关、path-size logit刻画路径重叠；对偶变量恢复偏好系数、特定方案常数及目的地/方式层尺度参数；观测出行模式以矩约束与条件熵约束引入，路径层离散参数用部分路段流量单独标定。

Result: 在八个基准网络上进行数值实验，验证了公式的可扩展性与计算效率，并可由现成锥优化求解器高效求解。

Conclusion: 该凸公式提供联合出行需求均衡与全局最优保证，为组合交通需求建模与统一参数估计提供可行途径。

Abstract: Travel demand forecasting is essential for evaluating large-scale infrastructure projects, yet the traditional sequential four-step process can produce inconsistencies across trip distribution, mode choice, and traffic assignment. Although combined models address these inconsistencies, their practical use has been limited by simplified behavioral assumptions, computational burden, and the lack of a unified parameter-estimation framework. We propose a convex programming approach that integrates destination, mode, and route choices within a hierarchical extended logit model. The optimal primal solution characterizes the joint travel-demand equilibrium, while the optimal dual variables recover taste coefficients, alternative-specific constants, and destination- and mode-level scale parameters. The model captures mode correlations through nested logit and route overlap through path-size logit. Its convex structure provides global optimality guarantees and enables efficient solution using off-the-shelf conic solvers. Observed travel patterns are incorporated through moment and conditional-entropy constraints, while the route-level dispersion parameter is calibrated separately using partial link counts. Numerical experiments on eight benchmark networks demonstrate the scalability and computational efficiency of the formulation.

</details>


### [302] [Equilibria of Round-Robin: Computational Hardness and Fairness for Few Subadditive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18309)
*Paul W. Goldberg,Alexandros Hollender,Giannis Tyrovolas*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究在线轮流挑选机制中次可加代理的策略行为，计算子博弈完美纳什均衡（SPNE）的复杂度，并给出规范性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮流挑选是简单且被广泛研究的公平分配机制，受体育联盟选秀机制启发，需理解代理在在线轮流挑选中的策略行为及其均衡性质。

Method: 将该过程建模为扩展式博弈，研究计算SPNE的计算复杂度；针对次模、OXS、加性等效用类进行分析，并研究均衡分配是否满足EF1、PROP1等公平性质。

Result: 仅两个次模代理时计算SPNE为PSPACE-hard；对OXS效用且代理数较少时仍为NP-hard。三个加性代理时存在所有均衡均违反EF1的实例，区分在线与直接显示博弈；加性代理下每个均衡分配满足PROP1，两个加性代理时还满足EF1；并证明均衡时轮流挑选是bossy的，即使词典偏好下均衡分配数量也可指数级。

Conclusion: 在线轮流挑选的SPNE计算在多种自然效用类下计算困难，且其均衡公平性与直接显示机制存在差异；正面结果给出PROP1及两代理EF1保证，但均衡分配数量可能指数级。

Abstract: The round-robin procedure is a simple and well-studied fair division mechanism where agents pick goods in turns. Motivated by draft mechanisms in sports leagues, we investigate strategic behaviour in online round-robin for subadditive agents. This gives rise to an extensive-form game, and we study the computational problem of computing a subgame perfect Nash equilibrium (SPNE). We show that for just two submodular agents, computing an SPNE is $\mathsf{PSPACE}$-hard. Even for the class of $\mathit{OXS}$ utilities, which are a special case of submodular utilities, computing an SPNE remains $\mathsf{NP}$-hard for a small number of agents. We complement our computational results with normative results. We show that for just three additive agents, there exist instances where every equilibrium violates EF1. This separates the online and the direct revelation games. On the positive side, we show that for additive agents every equilibrium allocation is proportional up to one good (PROP1) and for two additive agents it is also EF1. Finally, by showing that round-robin is bossy at equilibrium, we prove that the number of equilibrium allocations can be exponential even if agents have lexicographic preferences.

</details>


### [303] [Second-Best Gains from Trade in Matching Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18724)
*Xiaohui Bei,Bo Li,Wenhao Wu,Shengwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文证明在带独立私人类型和任意下闭可行性约束的双边匹配市场中，次优（BIC、间歇IR、强预算平衡）的预期贸易收益至少为最优收益的1/2，且该1/2比率在一般匹配市场中最坏情况下是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强预算平衡等约束通常使最优（first-best）贸易收益无法达到，因此需要量化效率损失；已有工作仅给出了双边交易的1/2保证，本文希望将其推广到多买方多卖方及任意下闭约束的匹配市场。

Method: 基于Brüstle等（EC 2017）的virtual-GFT框架，将问题化归为一参数拉格朗日问题；核心步骤是利用first-best边选择区域进行几何化的逐边分析，并结合秩空间中的随机收缩。

Result: 证明次优GFT ≥ 最优GFT的1/2，覆盖任意下闭可行性约束的匹配市场；结合Liu等（2026）双边交易的下界，得到该类市场最坏情况下1/2的紧比率。

Conclusion: 该结果恢复并推广了双边交易中的1/2保证，确立了该一般匹配市场类中最坏情况效率比率为1/2，并提供了基于虚GFT框架与几何分析的新证明思路。

Abstract: We study gains from trade (GFT) in two-sided matching markets with independent private types and arbitrary downward-closed feasibility constraints. The second-best benchmark is the maximum expected GFT achievable by a Bayesian incentive compatible, interim individually rational mechanism that is strongly budget balanced at every report profile. These constraints generally preclude attaining the first-best GFT and raise the question of how much efficiency must be lost. We prove that the second-best GFT is at least one half of the first-best GFT in every such matching market. This recovers and generalizes the recent $1/2$ guarantee for bilateral trade by Liu et al. (2026) to markets with multiple buyers and sellers and arbitrary downward-closed feasibility constraints. Together with their matching lower bound for bilateral trade, our result establishes a tight worst-case ratio of $1/2$ for this general class of matching markets. Our proof builds on the virtual-GFT framework of Brüstle et al. (EC 2017) to reduce the problem to a one-parameter Lagrangian. The main step is a geometric, edge-by-edge analysis based on first-best edge-selection regions, combined with a randomized contraction in rank space.

</details>


### [304] [Core stability recognition for minimum-cost spanning tree games: Parameterized perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18807)
*Michal Dvořák,Ioannis Kakatelis,Dušan Knop*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究最小生成树博弈(MSTG)中判断分配是否属于核心的计算复杂度，扩展了coNP-hard性并给出多种参数化算法与核化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MSTG核心成员判定问题在一般图中已知是coNP-hard，但对其在接近平面图上的难度以及可参数化算法和核化性质尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 将核心识别问题形式化为MSTG Core Non-Membership，使用参数化复杂度框架，围绕分配的支持大小、树宽、带符号邻域多样性、顶点覆盖数、反馈边数等参数设计FPT算法与核化。

Result: 将coNP-hard性扩展到非常接近平面的图；对支持大小、树宽和带符号邻域多样性给出FPT算法；一般图中顶点覆盖数参数下无多项式核(基于标准假设)，但在平面图中得到三次核；一般图中带符号邻域多样性得到二次核，反馈边数得到线性核。

Conclusion: MSTG核心非成员判定在接近平面图上仍困难，但在多个参数下具有良好的可解性，且核化行为因图结构和参数而异。

Abstract: Minimum-cost spanning tree game (MSTG) is a cooperative game played on an undirected edge-weighted graph $(G,w)$ representing the network, where each vertex corresponds to a player and each edge has an associated cost~$w$. A distinguished vertex $s \in V(G)$ represents the supply or source. For any coalition of players $S$, the characteristic cost function $c(S)$ is defined as the minimum cost of a spanning tree with respect to $w$, connecting exactly the vertices in $S \cup \{s\}$. In this paper we study the computational complexity of deciding core membership for MSTG. In general, deciding whether a given allocation is in the core is \textsf{coNP}-hard~(Faigle et al.,International Journal of Game Theory,1997). We study the core recognition problem under the name {\sc MSTG Core Non-Membership}. We extend the hardness to graphs which are very close to being planar. On the positive side, we present several algorithmic results within the framework of parameterized complexity. We show that {\sc MSTG Core Non-Membership} is fixed-parameter tractable when parameterized by the support size of the allocation. Turning into structural parameters of graphs, we show that the problem admits an FPT algorithm parameterized by treewidth and signed neighborhood diversity. Last but not least, we investigate kernelization. While in general graphs, under standard complexity-theoretical assumptions, {\sc MSTG Core Non-Membership} does not admit a polynomial kernel parameterized by the vertex cover number, we design a cubic kernel in planar graphs. Furthermore, in general graphs, we obtain quadratic kernel for signed neighborhood diversity and linear kernel for the parameter feedback edge number.

</details>


### [305] [Epsilon-Nash Equilibria in History-Dependent SA-MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18829)
*Brandon Gary Kaplowitz,Dominik Bohnet Zurcher,Akash Agrawal,Tala Jafari,Christian Schroeder de Witt,Paul W. Goldberg*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究状态对抗MDP（SA-MDP）在历史依赖策略下的均衡求解，证明不存在通用历史依赖均衡策略，并提出首个计算初始状态依赖ε-近似均衡的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SA-MDP工作聚焦马尔可夫策略，但历史依赖会实质改变均衡结果，迫使智能体与对手调整策略。

Method: 将SA-MDP归约为策略等价的约束零和单边部分可观测随机博弈，从而计算初始状态依赖均衡的ε-近似。

Result: 证明了通用（与初始状态分布无关）历史依赖均衡策略不存在；算法在小型可解析验证游戏中测试，并扩展到含12步前瞻的Atari Freeway等更大基准。

Conclusion: 揭示了历史依赖下SA-MDP均衡的复杂性，并提供了可行的近似计算路径。

Abstract: We study state-adversarial Markov decision processes (SA-MDP) as a game of observation-space attacks: at each step, an agent selects an action from a received observation while an adversary$\unicode{x2014}$who knows the true state the agent is in$\unicode{x2014}$chooses a perturbed observation within a state-dependent proximity set. While existing work focuses on Markovian policies, we develop a solution concept and computational approach for SA-MDPs under history dependence. This is motivated by results showing that history dependence can materially change equilibrium outcomes and can force both the agent and the adversary to adapt their strategies. First, we prove the non-existence of universal (agnostic of the initial state distribution) history-dependent equilibrium policies. In response to this finding, our main result presents the first algorithmic route to computing $ε$-approximations of initial-state dependent equilibria. We do so by reducing SA-MDPs to a strategically equivalent constrained zero-sum one-sided partially observable stochastic game. We conclude by testing our algorithm on small analytically verifiable games and showing it scales to larger, more realistic benchmarks, including Atari Freeway rollouts with a 12-period ahead horizon.

</details>


### [306] [On the Role of Tie-Breaking Rules in the Convergence of Fictitious Play for Symmetric First-Price Auctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18848)
*Benjamin Heymann*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究两投标人对称一级价格拍卖中连续时间虚拟博弈的收敛性，发现标准平局规则下不收敛，修改平局规则后可收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验虚拟博弈在拍卖场景中是否收敛到贝叶斯-纳什均衡，特别是标准平局规则下的稳定性问题。

Method: 研究两投标人、对称、独立离散估值与离散出价的连续时间虚拟博弈；构造最小反例，并修改平局规则（平局时收益为零）。

Result: 在最小实例（两投标人、两估值、三个正出价）中，标准均匀分配平局规则下均衡不稳定，动态收敛到远离纳什均衡的稳定极限环；改为平局零收益后，虚拟博弈收敛到修改博弈的纳什均衡。

Conclusion: 修改平局规则可恢复收敛，且该极限在广泛情形下是原拍卖的ε-均衡。

Abstract: We study continuous-time fictitious play in 2-bidder, symmetric first-price auctions with independently distributed discrete values and a discrete bid set. We first exhibit a minimal instance --- two bidders, two values, three positive bids --- on which fictitious play with the standard uniform-split tie-breaking rule does \emph{not} converge to the symmetric Bayes--Nash equilibrium: the equilibrium is unstable and the dynamics converge to a stable limit cycle far from the Nash equilibrium. We then show that a small modification of the tie-breaking rule --- awarding a payoff of zero to every bidder in case of a tie --- restores convergence: fictitious play converges to a Nash equilibrium of the modified game. This limit is an $ε$-equilibrium of the original auction in a broad range of settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [307] [SemABR: Measuring Video Semantic Fidelity with Multimodal LLMs for Adaptive Bitrate Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18075)
*Shiqi Xu,Soung Chang Liew,Yuyang Du*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出视频语义保真度(SF)指标，用MLLM+LLM评估压缩视频的语义保留，并嵌入5G MEC VoD资源分配框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频指标(PSNR/SSIM/VMAF)只衡量视觉失真，不直接反映语义保留；现有QoE驱动的码率选择与资源分配未显式考虑语义保留。

Method: 离线MLLM生成参考与压缩视频的结构化描述，单独的文本LLM评估语义对应度，生成依赖内容的SF-码率曲线并缓存；在线码率选择器查询缓存；将曲线嵌入5G MEC辅助VoD基站资源分配框架，联合选择各用户码率以减少语义损失。

Result: 三个主观QoE基准上SF与MOS正相关；独立人工语义评分研究中SF比传统指标与人类判断相关性更强；NS-3 5G NR仿真中取得更高平均与最差用户SF，且随每用户无线资源减少优势扩大。

Conclusion: SF能有效量化语义保留且吻合人类判断，将其嵌入资源分配可提升语义保真度，尤其适用于资源受限场景。

Abstract: Conventional video metrics such as PSNR, SSIM, and VMAF measure visual distortion or perceptual quality, but they do not directly capture semantic preservation: whether compression retains a video's objects, actions, and temporal narrative. Existing Quality-of-Experience (QoE)-driven bitrate-selection and resource-allocation methods primarily aim to minimize rebuffering and bitrate switching while maximizing perceptual video quality, without explicitly considering semantic preservation. To address this gap, we introduce video semantic fidelity (SF), a metric that quantifies how well a compressed video preserves the semantic content of its source. An offline multimodal large language model (MLLM) generates structured descriptions of the reference and compressed versions of the video, and a separate text-only large language model (LLM) evaluates their semantic correspondence. The resulting content-dependent SF--bitrate profiles are cached and queried by the online bitrate selector without invoking MLLMs at runtime. Evaluations on three subjective QoE benchmarks show a consistent positive association between SF and mean opinion scores (MOS). A separate human semantic-rating study evaluates semantic preservation and shows that SF correlates more strongly with human judgments than conventional video metrics. We then embed these profiles into a 5G MEC-assisted video-on-demand (VoD) resource-allocation framework at the base station. When wireless resources cannot support high bitrate levels for all users, the framework uses the SF--bitrate profiles to select bitrate levels jointly across users and reduce the semantic loss caused by the required bitrate reductions. NS-3 simulations with the 5G NR module show that the proposed framework achieves higher average and worst-user SF than the evaluated baselines, with a widening advantage as the wireless resources available to each user decrease.

</details>


### [308] [Multimodal Aspect-Level Sentiment Analysis Based on Gated Noise Filtering and Emotion-Relevance Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18404)
*Chen Huang,Liangwei Guo,Yamin Li,Yan Zhang,Chao Yang,Li Yang,Jianhua Song*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出GNSRI框架解决多模态方面级情感分析中的多模态噪声与跨模态情感对齐弱问题，在Twitter-2015/2017上分别提升1.94%和2.06%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多方面场景下存在两个挑战：(1)多模态噪声，方面无关内容干扰情感学习；(2)跨模态情感对齐弱，视觉证据模糊，文本与视觉情感可能冲突，限制多模态互补性。

Method: GNSRI框架包含：门控噪声过滤模块（抑制情感无关特征、增强方面感知情感线索）和情感相关性交互模块（在微观和宏观层面捕获一致与冲突的跨模态信号），并使用可学习决策融合机制在方面级别自适应组合文本、视觉与跨模态分支的预测。

Result: 在公开MABSA基准上超越SOTA，Twitter-2015准确率提升1.94%，Twitter-2017提升2.06%。

Conclusion: GNSRI通过噪声过滤与跨模态情感相关性交互有效提升多模态方面级情感分析性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (MABSA) infers fine-grained sentiment polarity toward specific aspects by jointly modeling text and images. Despite progress in cross-modal fusion, two challenges remain in multi-aspect settings: (1) multimodal noise, where aspect-irrelevant content distracts sentiment learning; and (2) weak cross-modal sentiment alignment, as visual evidence can be ambiguous and textual--visual sentiments may conflict, limiting multimodal complementarity. To address these issues, we propose a Gated Noise-filtered Sentiment-Relevance Interaction (GNSRI) framework. It employs a gated noise-filtering module to suppress sentiment-irrelevant features and enhance aspect-aware sentiment cues, and a sentiment-relevance interaction module to capture consistent and conflicting cross-modal signals at micro and macro levels. Finally, a learnable decision fusion mechanism adaptively combines predictions from textual, visual, and cross-modal branches at the aspect level. Experiments on public MABSA benchmarks show that GNSRI outperforms state-of-the-art methods, improving accuracy by 1.94\% and 2.06\% on Twitter-2015 and Twitter-2017, respectively.

</details>


### [309] [Divide and Conquer: Mixture-of-Bottleneck Experts in Informative Ordinal Space for Video-based Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18470)
*Ronghao Lin,Qiaolin He,Zefeng Lu,Yichu Liu,Li Huang,Sijie Mai,Haifeng Hu,Yap-peng Tan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 该论文将视频多模态情感分析重构为序数回归问题，并分解为极性识别与强度预测，提出基于信息瓶颈的混合瓶颈（MoB）框架，在4个数据集与4个语言模型上取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频多模态情感分析方法难以以任务感知方式整合多模态信息；多数模型将视频情感预测视为单一任务，忽视了其序数本质，且融合策略难以捕捉跨模态的独特与协同线索。

Method: 采用分而治之思路，将MSA重构为序数回归并解耦为极性识别和强度预测。基于信息论提出MoB框架，为不同模态的极性与强度专家分配不同隐变量；通过信息瓶颈学习，使各专家获得紧凑且任务相关的表示并过滤冗余噪声；再用多模态瓶颈路由融合模块结合难挖掘策略融合专家隐变量，在序数情感空间中引导预测。

Result: 在4个MSA数据集与4个语言模型上的大量实验表明，MoB能有效利用多模态信息性隐变量并捕捉通用情感结构；性能更强，且能全面捕捉细粒度的模态内与模态间动态，更可信地定位细微视频情感信号。

Conclusion: MoB通过任务感知的解耦与信息瓶颈驱动的融合，有效提升视频多模态情感分析性能与可解释性，证明序数回归视角与混合瓶颈设计的有效性。

Abstract: Video-based Multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) must handle information from text, audio, and image sequence in human speaking videos, yet current methods often fail to integrate modalities with task awareness. Most models treat video sentiment prediction as a single task, overlooking its ordinal nature, and their fusion strategies struggle to capture diverse unique and synergic cues across modalities. To address these limitations, we adopt a divide-and-conquer perspective by reformulating MSA as an ordinal regression problem and decoupling it into polarity recognition and intensity prediction. Driven by information theory, we introduce a Mixture-of-Bottleneck (MoB) framework that assigns different latents to polarity- and intensity-specific experts for different modalities. With the learning of information bottleneck, each expert learns compact and task-relevant representations while filtering out redundancy and noise. A multimodal bottleneck routing fusion module then fuses these expert latents with hard mining strategy, guiding the prediction in the ordinal sentiment space. Extensive experiments on 4 MSA datasets and 4 language models show that MoB effectively leverages informative latents from diverse modalities and captures general sentiment structure. Beyond stronger performance, MoB comprehensively captures fine-grained intra- and inter-modal dynamics, enabling more trustworthy localization of nuanced video sentiment signals.

</details>


### [310] [MoQSplat: Adaptive Progressive Streaming of 3D Gaussian Splatting via MoQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18624)
*Emanuele Artioli,Mohammadreza Ghafari,Md Tariqul Islam,Farzad Tashtarian,Christian Rothenberg,Christian Timmerer*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出MoQSplat，将3DGS内容映射到MoQ传输层级，通过空间Track分组与独立QUIC流消除队头阻塞，实现6-DoF驱动的自适应渐进式传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽能实现逼真新视角合成，但场景数据达GB级，沉浸式传输困难。传统基于TCP的HTTP自适应流存在队头阻塞和粗粒度分段问题，不适合细粒度3DGS传输。

Method: 将3DGS映射到Media over QUIC (MoQ)层级：把场景划分为空间Track，将splat聚类为语义连贯的Group，构建渐进质量Subgroup并映射到独立QUIC流以消除连接级HOL阻塞；采用无状态、订阅者驱动的自适应循环，客户端基于6-DoF视锥可见性、距离和中央凹对齐动态请求空间区域与质量层级。

Result: 在原型实现上评估核心组件，显示基于不透明度的剪枝在渐进式传输上优于基于尺度的剪枝。

Conclusion: MoQSplat利用MoQ与QUIC特性有效解决3DGS传输的HOL阻塞与粒度问题，不透明度剪枝更适合渐进式交付；代码已开源。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables photorealistic novel view synthesis, but transmitting gigabyte-scale scene data remains challenging for immersive applications. Traditional HTTP Adaptive Streaming over TCP introduces Head-of-Line (HOL) blocking and coarse segmenting ill-suited to fine-grained 3DGS delivery. We propose MoQSplat, which maps 3DGS content onto the Media over QUIC (MoQ) transport hierarchy. MoQSplat partitions scenes into spatial Tracks, clusters splats into semantically coherent Groups, and constructs progressive-quality Subgroups mapped to independent QUIC streams to eliminate connection-level HOL blocking. Using a stateless, subscriber-driven adaptation loop, clients dynamically request spatial regions and quality tiers based on six degrees of freedom (6-DoF) frustum visibility, distance, and foveal alignment. We evaluate the core components on a prototype implementation, showing that opacity-based pruning outperforms scale-based pruning for progressive delivery. The source code is available at https://github.com/emanuele-artioli/MoQSplat.

</details>


### [311] [Transcribe, Then Reason: Two-Pass Decomposition for Multimodal Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18958)
*Bojie Li,Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文揭示多模态模型一次性审阅长内容时会"偷工减料"（丢失约1/3内容并添油加醋），原因是生成负载瓶颈而非感知能力；提出"先转录后审阅"的两步分解方法可提升忠实度与覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接让多模态模型一次性审阅长录音/文档会静默失败：丢失内容、编造信息。作者欲探究失败根源并寻找解决方案。

Method: 通过实验排除多种假设（非感知问题、非模态问题、非推理预算问题），提出将任务分解为两个相同权重的处理步骤：先转录，再基于转录进行审阅，使每步拥有独立完整的输出预算。

Result: 在21个源数据的测试集上，转录-审阅分解显著提升忠实度与覆盖率；效果不均衡——在单步基线最弱处提升最大，与源长度和模态相关。发现两种失败模式：超长源时审阅步骤空间不足，以及移除源后从记忆编造。

Conclusion: 一次性审阅的瓶颈在于单次生成需同时承担感知、推理与长文写作三任务，共享输出预算。分解为转录+审阅可有效缓解，但需注意长源空间限制与记忆幻觉两个局限。

Abstract: The natural way to review a long recording or document with a multimodal model is to hand it the raw source and ask for a review in one call. We show that this quietly fails: the model satisfices, dropping roughly a third of the content and embellishing the rest. The failure is not perception--almost all of the dropped content reappears when the same model is simply asked to transcribe the source. The bottleneck is generation under load: a single pass cannot perceive, reason over, and write a long faithful review at the same time, because doing all three competes for one output. We rule out the obvious alternatives. It is not the modality: models read text and an image of the same text equally well. And it is not merely a matter of thinking harder: giving the single pass a far larger reasoning budget does not recover the lost content, because the model spends that budget planning a review rather than writing the source down. What works is to split the labor across two same-weights passes--first transcribe, then review the transcript--so each step gets a full output budget of its own. This transcribe-then-review decomposition improves both faithfulness and coverage across a 21-source suite. The benefit is not uniform: we observe that it helps most where the one-pass baseline is weakest and little where that baseline is already strong, a pattern that also tracks the source's length and modality. Decomposition comes with two failure modes--the review pass running out of room on very long sources, and confabulating from memory once the grounding source is removed.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [312] [Pay Only for Disagreement: Certified No-Regression Verdicts for Model Updates with Matching Label-Complexity Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17560)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将模型更新上线形式化为"配对风险差审计"，利用支持集恒等式（风险差只存在于两模型预测不一致的输入上）构建 DISCERN 两阶段序贯协议：无标签层仅凭无标签流量认证良性更新，审计层仅对有分歧样本打标签并用随时有效置信序列保证任意停止时刻有效，理论上节省 1/rho 标签量，实验覆盖 785 对更新的 1.4 万条审计流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产模型会因重训练、微调、量化或供应商静默替换而不断更新，每次更新都可能比被替换的旧模型更差，但缺少能给出可验证保证的更新上线（promotion）审计方法，尤其是无标签场景下如何廉价地判断更新是否良性。

Method: 从"支持集恒等式"出发：两模型风险差只集中在预测不一致的输入上，且该集合无需标签即可观测。据此设计 DISCERN 序贯两阶段协议：(1) 零标签层，用无标签流量测量分歧率，低于容差即认证为良性；(2) 审计层，仅对采样到的分歧样本打标签，采用随时有效（anytime-valid）置信序列，保证在任意停止时刻及任意标签路由规则（甚至对抗性裁判）下有效。同时给出有限样本有效性与阶为 rho^2/eps^2 的标签复杂度匹配界。

Result: 在 785 对更新、14,000+ 条回放审计流（含最高 1.4B 参数的 LoRA 微调语言模型）上：误覆盖率 0.0002（标称 5%），功效 0.986 且零误报，56% 的良性更新以零标签完成认证。每次审计输出可供机器校验的证据记录，用于上市后监测。

Conclusion: DISCERN 将模型更新上线转化为可认证的配对风险差审计问题，证明利用"免费的分歧信息"可比任何配对盲审计器在标签复杂度上节省 1/rho 因子，且保证可在单一误差预算下跨无界次数的更新序列组合传递。

Abstract: Every production model is updated, by retraining, fine-tuning, quantization, or a silent vendor swap, and each update risks being worse than what it replaced. We formalize update promotion as certified paired risk-difference auditing. Our starting point is a support identity: the risk difference between two models lives on the inputs where they disagree, observable without labels. We build DISCERN, a sequential two-tier protocol. A zero-label tier certifies benign updates whose disagreement rate is below tolerance from unlabeled traffic alone. An audited tier labels only sampled disagreements through an anytime-valid confidence sequence, valid at every stopping time and under any label-routing rule, even an adversarial judge. We prove finite-sample validity and matching label-complexity bounds of order rho^2/eps^2 at the rate level, so exploiting free disagreement provably saves a factor 1/rho over any pairing-blind auditor, and the guarantee composes across an unbounded sequence of promotions from one error budget. Across 14,000+ replayed audit streams over 785 update pairs, including LoRA fine-tunes of language models up to 1.4B parameters, miscoverage is 0.0002 (nominal 5%), power 0.986 with zero false alarms, and 56% of benign updates certify with zero labels. Each audit emits a machine-checkable evidence record for post-market monitoring.

</details>


### [313] [Beyond Static RAG: An Adaptive, Tri-Metric Routing Framework for Efficient Long-Context Inference on Commodity GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17564)
*Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Ajay Kumar Boddepalli,Deep Narayan Mishra,Shashank Kapadia,Haoan Wang,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在T4等低显存GPU上部署RAG时，神经prompt压缩可能与KV cache争抢显存导致"压缩悖论"。作者提出Tri-Metric Router，用三个CPU侧信号做免训练的无参路由，在Raw/Neural/BM25三种pipeline间选择，实现0% OOM和更高F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低显存GPU（如16GB的NVIDIA T4）上部署RAG存在"压缩悖论"：神经prompt压缩会加剧KV cache争用和预处理延迟，抵消生成端收益；但不压缩又会在长上下文时OOM。作者识别出vLLM LLM与PyTorch压缩器共部署时的两种失败机制。

Method: 提出Tri-Metric Router，一种确定性、免训练的策略，在Raw、Neural（LLMLingua-2）、Lexical（BM25）三种pipeline间选择。使用三个CPU侧信号：空间复杂度L、句法密度ρ_key和type-token ratio (TTR)。不同于以往纯语义适配，其调度信号基于硬件物理特性（VRAM余量与延迟交叉点）。阈值在LongBench qasper上profiling标定，T4上交叉点约4,332词。

Result: 在分布外留出集上：0% OOM失败，88.5±4.4% oracle对齐，49.3% Combined F1，比always-on lexical compression提升5.2个百分点，且不增加VRAM或训练成本。

Conclusion: 贡献在于标定方法学而非硬件特定常数；通过硬件物理驱动的路由信号，在低显存GPU上平衡了压缩收益与OOM风险。

Abstract: Deploying retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) on commodity GPUs such as the NVIDIA T4 (16 GB VRAM) exposes a practical failure mode we call the Compression Paradox: neural prompt compression can add key-value (KV) cache contention and preprocessing latency that outweigh generation-time savings, while skipping compression can cause out-of-memory (OOM) failures on long contexts. We identify two distinct failure mechanisms when a vLLM-served LLM and a PyTorch-based compressor are co-deployed under tight memory budgets, and introduce the Tri-Metric Router, a deterministic, training-free policy that selects among Raw, Neural (LLMLingua-2), and Lexical (BM25) pipelines. The router uses three CPU-side signals: spatial complexity ($L$), syntactic density ($ρ_{key}$), and type-token ratio (TTR). Unlike prior semantic-only adaptation, our dispatch signal is hardware-physical, based on VRAM headroom and a latency crossover point. Thresholds are calibrated from profiling on LongBench qasper, yielding an operating crossover near 4,332 words on T4; our contribution is this calibration methodology rather than a hardware-specific constant. On out-of-distribution holdouts, the method achieves 0% OOM failures, 88.5 $\pm$ 4.4% oracle alignment, and 49.3% Combined F1, improving over always-on lexical compression by 5.2 points without additional VRAM or training cost.

</details>


### [314] [Where Grokking Happens: Distributed Utility and Fourier Recoding Without a Module Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17571)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Transition Games方法，定位Transformer中从记忆到泛化转变的功能表达，发现grokking是已有分布式电路的谱重编码而非模块切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer中记忆到泛化的转变在何处功能性地体现，并检验"MLP记忆、注意力泛化"这一流行预测是否正确。

Method: 引入Transition Games——行为对齐的精确激活博弈，配合成对的非泛化对照组；并进行替换博弈与精确路径分析。

Result: 发现分布式效用增益与前瞻性block-0注意力偏置；被选二阶模式在替换博弈中贡献67–92%的加法对比；精确路径研究确认block-1 MLP在12/12对中比所有其他下游路径介导更多效应。而"MLP记忆、注意力泛化"预测被反转（记忆锚点处-.331 bits/example，0/12符合预测方向）；路由起始、全局秩崩塌、质数不变架构脊均失败。

Conclusion: 此处的grokking是已有分布式电路的谱重编码，而非模块切换。

Abstract: Where in a Transformer is the change from memorization to generalization functionally expressed? We introduce Transition Games--behavior-aligned exact activation games with paired non-generalizing controls--and find distributed utility gain with a prospective block-0 attention bias; selected degree-two modes account for 67--92% of its addition contrast across replacement games, and a disjoint exact path study confirms that block-1 MLP mediates more of their effect than all other tested downstream paths in 12/12 pairs. The sharper "MLP memorizes, attention generalizes" prediction instead reverses (-.331 bits/example at the memory anchor; 0/12 in the predicted direction), while routing onset, global rank collapse, and a prime-invariant architecture ridge also fail, identifying grokking here as spectral recoding of an existing distributed circuit rather than a module switch.

</details>


### [315] [Disentangling Algorithmic Bias from Archival Artifacts: A Controlled Audit of Vision-Language Model Valuation in Metropolitan Museum Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17572)
*Manpreet Singh,Rhythm Bhatia,Rahul Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审计CLIP模型在历史艺术品元数据中的社会偏见，发现性别主效应不显著（经TOST确认等价），但指出零样本评分是粗粒度指标，存在测量不敏感和档案存活偏差的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计视觉语言模型的社会偏见时，需区分算法本身的估值差异与档案元数据中的混杂因素（如媒介、年代、比例、匿名/归属偏差）。

Method: 使用大都会艺术博物馆开放数据（N=1500，743件署名作品；男534、女209，匿名618）。构建定量审计框架，对三个语义提示对（杰作、质量、影响力）计算零样本CLIP logit差分分数，进行未调整比较、TOST等价检验及控制媒介/年代/长宽比的多元OLS回归。

Result: 未调整结果显示性别主效应不显著（OpenAI CLIP p=0.1829；OpenCLIP p=0.1224），TOST确认在Cohen's d≥0.25内等价（pTOST<0.005）。OLS回归中性别条件效应不显著（p>0.20），R²<0.02，残余方差高，表明指标接近嵌入噪声下限。

Conclusion: 宏观评分等价反映指标不敏感，不能证明绝对公平，不排除微观视觉偏差；排除41.2%未署名藏品存在机构存活偏差。评估文化遗产AI公平性须结合多元混杂控制、等价检验与档案来源审计。

Abstract: Auditing vision-language models (VLMs) for societal bias requires distinguishing direct algorithmic valuation disparities from confounders embedded within archival metadata. In this study, we audit Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models using historical artwork metadata from the Metropolitan Museum of Art Open Access collection (N = 1,500 total objects; N = 743 attributed works: Male n = 534, Female n = 209; n = 618 anonymous).
  We establish a quantitative audit framework evaluating zero-shot CLIP logit differential scores across three semantic prompt pairs (masterpiece, quality, and influence). Unadjusted evaluations demonstrate high score convergence without a statistically significant main gender effect under OpenAI CLIP (mu_F = -0.0067 vs mu_M = -0.0035, p = 0.1829) or OpenCLIP (mu_F = 0.0171 vs mu_M = 0.0237, p = 0.1224). Two One-Sided Tests (TOST) confirm statistical equivalence across Cohen's d >= 0.25 bounds (pTOST < 0.005).
  Multivariate OLS regression controlling for artwork medium, creation era, and aspect ratio (R^2 < 0.02) confirms that artist gender has no statistically significant conditional effect (p > 0.20). High residual embedding variance (R^2 < 2%) indicates that global zero-shot valuation metrics operate near an embedding noise floor, showing that broad zero-shot prompt logit differentials are a coarse measurement instrument rather than proving absolute model fairness.
  We highlight two key caveats: (i) macro-level score equivalence reflects metric insensitivity to fine-grained visual-semantic features and does not preclude localized micro-level visual biases, and (ii) excluding 41.2% unattributed holdings reflects institutional survival bias. These results demonstrate the necessity of multivariate confound control, equivalence testing, and archival provenance auditing when assessing AI fairness in cultural heritage collections.

</details>


### [316] [Temperon: Full-Time SAM Quality at a Third Less Wall-Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17575)
*Stamatis Mastromichalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Temperon：前43%训练用普通SGD探索，最后cosine退火阶段交给SAM包裹的Muon精修，兼顾精度与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM每步训练成本翻倍，但其收益主要集中在训练末尾，因此应研究"昂贵的训练模式该花在哪里"。

Method: Temperon：前43% epoch用普通SGD探索，随后一次既定切换，将整个最终cosine退火交给SAM+Muon精修器。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100、SVHN、Tiny ImageNet上，精度持平最佳全时SAM，三个数据集上最快达到最难目标分别快35%/34%/32%；消融显示Muon精修贡献+0.85pp；迁移到GPT-2预训练(-29%时间)与GLUE微调。Muon一个epoch成本为SAM+SGD的1.5x。

Conclusion: 训练模式分配应遵循计划而非轨迹，切换点由cosine调度决定，方法在Muon有效时收益显著，并有明确适用边界。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) doubles the cost of every training step, yet its benefit concentrates where training ends. We study where an expensive training mode should be spent and propose Temperon: a plain-SGD explorer for the first 43% of the epoch budget, then one scheduled hand-off that gives the entire final cosine anneal to a SAM-wrapped Muon refiner. On CIFAR-10/100, SVHN and Tiny ImageNet (five seeds, times reported as epochs-to-target times an idle-GPU-calibrated epoch cost), Temperon matches the best full-time-SAM recipe on accuracy everywhere while reaching the hardest common target 35%, 34% and 32% sooner on three of the four, and sits a tier above the published SAM+SGD recipe at level cost. Ablations make the attribution exact: the Muon refiner is worth +0.85pp with everything else fixed; the explorer's shape and its restarts are worth nothing, and we withdraw them as contributions. Re-running the closest rival, late-phase SAM, at matched budget shows the frontier: it is fastest to every mid-level target, but the tier the Muon refiner buys (0.83 on CIFAR-100, 0.97 on CIFAR-10) is reached by no SGD-refined method in any seed, and on Tiny ImageNet, where Muon buys no tier, the rival simply wins -- the measured boundary of the method. The allocation law transfers to GPT-2 pretraining (full-SAM quality at -29% wall-clock) and GLUE fine-tuning (never worse than full-time SAM at a third of its SAM cost). Two constants organize the economics: skipping SAM early buys a fixed credit, and a Muon epoch costs 1.50x a SAM+SGD epoch on all four datasets. Finally, the hand-off cannot be timed from the trajectory: under cosine schedules the accuracy curve is plateau-then-surge, so the information lives in the schedule, making the scheduled switch principled rather than convenient. Code and a pip-installable implementation are released.

</details>


### [317] [Transformation Laws in Neural Representations: Structure, Realisability, and Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18190)
*Yuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经表征中参考变换的"可操作内容"，刻画变换何时能通过编码器下降，并给出线性设定下的缺陷度量；以颜色（色相）为实例，发现冻结视觉特征中色相轨道能量集中于前两个谐波，据此构建零样本色相读取接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接表征分析与内部干预：希望理解神经表征如何保留输入变换的结构，从而实现可控的（可操作的）表征内容，而非仅做被动分析。

Method: 形式化刻画变换何时"下降"通过编码器；给出线性设定，用表征诱导的度量衡量被丢弃信息的需求；在整流器上区分两类失败来源，在 measured harmonic carrier 上给出闭式条件；以颜色为深度实例，测量冻结视觉特征中色相轨道的谐波结构、训练/深度影响，并据此构造旋转作用固定的紧凑接口。

Result: 色相轨道 84–88% 能量集中在前两个谐波且旋转平面跨形状共享；该组织主要继承自输入与架构，并受训练与深度重塑；测得结构支持预测、从新起点迁移与组合，全局与局部实现差异明显；构建的接口无需拟合旋转作用，在未见形状上零样本色相读取中位误差 3.4°。

Conclusion: 理论、结构测量与构造三者共同将"变换律"确立为连接神经表征理解与设计的具体对象。

Abstract: How neural representations preserve the structure of input changes connects representation analysis with internal intervention. We study operable representational content through compatible actions of reference transformations on neural features. We characterise when a transformation descends through an encoder, and give a linear setting in which the defect is governed by the transformation's demand for discarded information, measured in the metric the representation induces. On a rectifier the failure to realise a transformation has two distinguishable sources --- what the source region has already made unrecoverable, and what it costs to satisfy every region the transformation visits with one operator --- and for a \textit{measured} harmonic carrier the same question has a closed answer: a linear realisation exists exactly when the retained harmonic blocks are invariant under the action. Using colour as the in-depth instance, we find that hue orbits in frozen visual features concentrate 84--88\% of their energy in the first two harmonics with rotation planes shared across shapes, that this organisation is substantially inherited from input and architecture and is reshaped by training and depth, and that the measured structure supports prediction, transport from new starting states, and composition --- with global and local realisations differing sharply in which they achieve. Guided by the measurements, we construct a compact interface whose rotation action is fixed by the structure and never fitted: it reads hue zero-shot at 3.4$^\circ$ median error on unseen shapes. Theory, structural measurement, and construction together establish transformation laws as a concrete object connecting the understanding of neural representations to their design.

</details>


### [318] [When the Gradient Sees Rank: Provable Necessity, Causal Recruitment, and Composition in Trained Matrix Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17594)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究矩阵记忆的梯度训练能否学到存储与组合关联所需的秩，发现学习到的有效秩随K增长，秩上限在k=K附近产生恢复相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的矩阵增强推理器任务只容许秩1解，无法回答梯度训练能否学到存储K个键值绑定所需的秩（需rank(Z)≥K），因此本文针对该开放问题展开研究。

Method: 在K个新键值绑定上训练矩阵记忆，精确线性恢复要求rank(Z)≥K；用固定线性读出在无原始绑定访问下查询单一矩阵状态，以余弦相似度>0.9衡量恢复，并设置训练时秩上限。

Result: 学习到的有效秩随K增加（d=16时Spearman ρ=1.0）；秩上限在k=K附近产生恢复相变（d=8,K=4时秩3恢复≤0.0004，秩4达0.97）；5个种子中4个经21次自应用仍保持≥0.9996恢复；实体子空间上学到的算子有效秩接近K并近似理想循环；对收敛但秩低于K的种子，预测余弦误差在7次应用内<0.008。

Conclusion: 梯度训练能学到与K匹配的有效秩并支持长程自组合，但仍受编码器宽度固定时矩阵维度增大导致的恢复下降限制；部分初始失败经延长训练可解决。

Abstract: Can gradient-based training learn the rank needed to store and compose associations in a matrix memory? In our earlier study, we used a matrix-augmented reasoner on a task that admits a rank-1 solution, leaving this question open. We train matrix memories on $K$ fresh key-value bindings whose exact linear recovery requires $\mathrm{rank}(Z) \geq K$. A fixed linear readout queries a single matrix state without access to the original bindings. Experiments measure recovery by cosine similarity greater than 0.9, a threshold distinct from mathematical equality. Learned effective rank increases with $K$ across the tested grid (Spearman $ρ= 1.0$ at $d = 16$). Training-time rank caps produce a recovery transition near $k = K$: at $d = 8$, $K = 4$, rank 3 gives at most 0.0004 recovery and rank 4 gives 0.97. Four of five seeds retain at least 0.9996 recovery through 21-fold self-application of the trained operator. On the entity subspace, the learned operator has effective rank close to $K$ and approximates the ideal cycle. For the single converged seed capped below $K$, a calculation using the entity-subspace operator and ideal cycle predicts the measured cosine within 0.008 through seven applications. Extending training resolves several initial failures, but recovery still declines at larger matrix dimensions with encoder width fixed.

</details>


### [319] [The evolution of sex for artificial intelligence: a population-genetic framework for multigenerational model populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18560)
*Giorgio F. Gilestro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将AI多代模型演化类比为群体遗传学中的繁殖过程，用Wright-Fisher过程、混合遗传等框架解释模型坍缩、数据迁移和模型合并等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型常基于前代输出再训练、平均权重或专业化组合，形成跨代模型群体；作者希望借用群体遗传学为这种“AI社会”建立可预测的继承理论。

Method: 建立精确继承模型，将再训练类比有性/无性繁殖；在循环网络、前馈网络、VAE生成器和大型语言模型上检验；用Wright-Fisher过程描述模型坍缩，引入真实数据作为“移民”，并比较平均合并与保留最强贡献的合并方式。

Result: 再训练父母的输出精确复现Wright-Fisher过程；真实数据样本的绝对数量而非比例决定效果；平均父母输出会像混合遗传一样抵消多亲优势，而保留各自最强贡献可保留优势；合并语言模型专家在多种子下超过所有父母，体现Fisher-Muller效应；当学习到冲突约定而非仅漂移时，谱系会生殖隔离并失去合并能力。

Conclusion: AI模型群体在时间维度上也可视为社会，其继承规律可大量借用生物学框架；该数学框架具有预测能力，但存在可测量的架构特异性偏差。

Abstract: Some aspects of AI development resemble a population process in which models are specialised, retrained on the output of peers, or combined by averaging weights. These practices lead to generations of models, in the biological sense studied by population genetics. Here, I develop this parallelism and interpret multigenerational model populations in terms of sexual and asexual reproduction, formally recombining the two fields. I test these analogies in an exact inheritance model, in trained networks (recurrent, feedforward and variational autoencoder generators) and in large language models, and show that they hold generally, with some measurable architecture-specific biases.
  Training recursively on model output is known to lead to model collapse, a process previously described as akin to genetic drift; I develop all that follows. A minimal model of a learner retrained on its parent's output reproduces the Wright-Fisher process exactly; verified real data added to each generation play the role of immigration, with the surprising finding that the absolute number of real data samples matters, not their share, exactly as in population genetics. Training a child on the average of its parents' outputs cancels the benefit of having several parents, matching blending inheritance (and reviving Jenkin's objection to Darwin), whereas combining parents so that each keeps its strongest contribution preserves it; merged language-model specialists exceeded every parent across seeds (the Fisher-Muller effect); and lineages become reproductively isolated, losing the ability to merge at all, when they have learned conflicting conventions and not when they have merely drifted apart.
  As AI societies become societies in time as well as in space, a mathematical framework for their inheritance acquires predictive power. Remarkably, that framework can be adapted almost wholesale from biology.

</details>


### [320] [Prior-Free Competitive Ratios for Improving Bandits: Scale, Curvature and Horizon Are Free, but Not Jointly Under Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17595)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究改进型多臂老虎机问题，去除现有结果中的对数因子，证明最优竞争比为 Θ(√k+k/T)，并给出无需先验知识的探测-承诺算法及噪声下的适应代价分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blum与Ravichandran (ALT 2025) 的结果含多余的对数因子（O(√k log k)），且依赖对规模m或凹包指数β的先验知识，而理论下界仅为Ω(√k)。本文旨在消除该对数因子、确定各horizon下的最优比率，并研究无先验知识时的适应代价。

Method: 提出一页的"探测-承诺"（probe-and-commit）算法：无需知道规模m即可达到竞争比4√3√k（T≥2⌊√k⌋）。在无噪声情形，采用随机边缘探测算法，不需读取规模m、凹包指数β或horizon T。在乘性噪声模型下，用嵌套随机排列探测算法匹配下界。

Result: 确定每个horizon的最优比率为Θ(√k+k/T)（含未知horizon）。无噪声时，随机边缘探测算法同时对所有β和horizon达到最优Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T)。噪声下，probe-and-commit保持Θ(√k+k/T)且不需知道噪声水平；均匀适应代价φ_ε(k)=Θ_ε(√(log k/log log k))，而知道m或β任一即可恢复常数代价。

Conclusion: 对数因子本质上是可去除的；无需任何先验知识的算法即可达到最优比率。噪声显著提高适应的代价：从无噪声的常数代价跃升为Θ_ε(√(log k/log log k))，而部分先验（m或β）可消除该代价。

Abstract: In the improving multi-armed bandits problem, each of $k$ arms has an unknown nondecreasing, discretely concave reward curve $f_i$, and pulling arm $i$ for the $t$-th time yields $f_i(t)$. For sufficiently long horizons, Blum and Ravichandran (ALT 2025) proved that randomized algorithms achieve an $O(\sqrt k)$ approximation to the best single arm when the scale $m=f^*(T)$ of the optimal arm is known ($T\ge2k$), and $O(\sqrt k\log k)$ when it is not ($T>4k$), against an $Ω(\sqrt k)$ lower bound. The logarithmic factor is unnecessary: a one-page \emph{probe-and-commit} algorithm achieves competitive ratio $4\sqrt3\,\sqrt k$ for $T\ge2\lfloor\sqrt k\rfloor$, without any knowledge of the scale, and we determine the optimal ratio for every horizon, $Θ(\sqrt k+k/T)$, also for unknown horizons. Without noise, \emph{no prior is needed at all}: a random-marginal probing algorithm reading neither the scale $m$, nor the concavity-envelope exponent $β$ of Blum, Garicano, Ravichandran and Sharma (UAI 2026), nor the horizon $T$, achieves the optimal $Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T)$ simultaneously for every $β$ and every horizon. Under the multiplicative noise model of Blum and Ravichandran, probe-and-commit keeps the same all-horizon order $Θ(\sqrt k+k/T)$ without knowing the noise level (and $Θ(\sqrt k)$ on the same range), but the price of priors jumps: for any fixed noise level $\varepsilon\in(0,1/2]$, the uniform price of adaptation $φ_\varepsilon(k)$ --- the worst case over horizons $T\ge16k$ of the loss relative to $k^{β/(1+β)}$ for algorithms knowing neither $m$ nor $β$ --- is $Θ_\varepsilon(\sqrt{\log k/\log\log k})$, the lower bound asymptotic in $k$ at fixed positive $\varepsilon$ and matched by a nested random-permutation probing algorithm, whereas knowing either $m$ or $β$ alone restores a constant price.

</details>


### [321] [On Predicting Post-Click Conversion Rate via Counterfactual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04816)
*Junhyung Ahn,Sanghack Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ESCIM模型，利用因果推断为未点击样本生成反事实转化标签，缓解CVR预测中点击样本稀疏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVR预测通常仅用点击样本训练，但点击样本稀疏，需大量日志。现有利用未点击样本的方法多依赖启发式，缺乏原则性指导。

Method: 构建用户序列行为的结构因果模型(SCM)，对未点击项施加假设干预(点击)，推断反事实CVR；再用多种方法将预测的反事实CVR转为二元反事实转化标签，加入训练。模型称为ESCIM(全空间反事实推断多任务模型)。

Result: 在公开数据集上实验显示算法优越；线上A/B测试验证真实场景有效性；在潜在转化数据上表现提升，展示稳健性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 以因果为原则生成未点击样本反事实标签，可有效提升CVR预测性能，并在实际应用与潜在转化场景中具备稳健性和泛化性。

Abstract: Accurately predicting conversion rate (CVR) is essential in various recommendation domains such as online advertising systems and e-commerce. These systems utilize user interaction logs, which consist of exposures, clicks, and conversions. CVR prediction models are typically trained solely based on clicked samples, as conversions can only be determined following clicks. However, the sparsity of clicked instances necessitates the collection of a substantial amount of logs for effective model training. Recent works address this issue by devising frameworks that leverage non-clicked samples. While these frameworks aim to reduce biases caused by the discrepancy between clicked and non-clicked samples, they often rely on heuristics. Against this background, we propose a method to counterfactually generate conversion labels for non-clicked samples by using causality as a guiding principle, attempting to answer the question, "Would the user have converted if he or she had clicked the recommended item?" Our approach is named the Entire Space Counterfactual Inference Multi-task Model (ESCIM). We initially train a structural causal model (SCM) of user sequential behaviors and conduct a hypothetical intervention (i.e., click) on non-clicked items to infer counterfactual CVRs. We then introduce several approaches to transform predicted counterfactual CVRs into binary counterfactual conversion labels for the non-clicked samples. Finally, the generated samples are incorporated into the training process. Extensive experiments on public datasets illustrate the superiority of the proposed algorithm. Online A/B testing further empirically validates the effectiveness of our proposed algorithm in real-world scenarios. In addition, we demonstrate the improved performance of the proposed method on latent conversion data, showcasing its robustness and superior generalization capabilities.

</details>


### [322] [ReDIL-GNN: Resynthesis Domain Incremental Learning for Circuit Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18595)
*Rupesh Raj Karn,Johann Knechtel,Ozgur Sinanoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出ReDIL-GNN框架，用于电路逻辑重合成导致的域增量学习问题，并引入RAI指标判断是否值得适配新合成风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑重合成会改变门词汇、拓扑和结构统计，导致电路GNN出现域偏移但任务标签不变，因此需要研究如何适配新合成风格同时保持旧域性能。

Method: 提出ReDIL-GNN域增量学习框架，适配固定预测或表示头并评估旧域保留；同时提出RAI预适配评分，结合适配需求、源等价可恢复性、结构覆盖和更新兼容性。对比朴素微调及LwF、Online EWC、MAS、ER、A-GEM、DER++、ER+LwF、等价引导回放。

Result: 在硬件安全分类任务和表示学习模型上，RAI能区分不支持与有前景的偏移，得分从结构未覆盖GNN-RE ABC重写偏移的0.001到最佳仅原始域GNN-RE适配的0.824。

Conclusion: ReDIL-GNN将重合成感知电路学习转化为部署控制循环：RAI在更新前筛选新合成流程，决定复用当前模型、进行保留感知适配，或延迟适配。

Abstract: Logic resynthesis preserves circuit functionality while changing gate vocabulary, topology, and structural statistics, creating domain shift for circuit graph neural networks (GNNs) without changing task labels. To study this setting, we introduce ReDIL-GNN, a resynthesis domain-incremental learning framework that adapts a fixed prediction or representation head as new synthesis styles arrive and evaluates retention on all previously observed domains. Because not every shift should be adapted blindly, ReDIL-GNN further introduces the Resynthesis Adaptability Index (RAI), a pre-adaptation score that combines adaptation need, source-equivalence recoverability, structural coverage, and update compatibility. We evaluate supervised hardware-security tasks and representation-learning models using task-native metrics for classifiers and source-equivalence retrieval metrics for embedding models, comparing naive fine-tuning with LwF, Online EWC, MAS, ER, A-GEM, DER++, ER+LwF, and equivalence-guided replay. Across the studied pipelines, RAI separates unsupported shifts from promising updates, ranging from 0.001 for a structurally uncovered GNN-RE ABC-rewrite shift to 0.824 for the best original-only GNN-RE adaptation case. In practice, ReDIL-GNN turns resynthesis-aware circuit learning into a deployment control loop: RAI screens each new synthesis flow before update, guiding whether to reuse the current model, apply retention-aware adaptation, or defer adaptation until the shift is better supported.

</details>


### [323] [Lecture notes on Physics Informed Neural Networks, Neural Operators, and their applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17638)
*Alessandro Bombini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一门关于物理信息神经网络（PINN）与神经算子（NO）的博士课程讲义，涵盖从零实现、开源库应用及前沿主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程旨在引入物理信息深度神经网络（PINN）与神经算子（NO）的概念，弥合深度学习与实际物理/工程问题求解之间的鸿沟。

Method: 通过PyTorch从零实现这些模型，并使用如NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo等专用开源库，将其应用于工程、物理、石油储层等真实问题。

Result: 课程内容涵盖近期热点方法，包括混合模型（Mixture-of-Models）、傅里叶神经算子（FNO）以及物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（PIKANs）。

Conclusion: 该讲义为读者提供了系统学习PINN与NO的理论基础、实现手段及在多个领域的实际应用案例。

Abstract: This is the set of lecture notes for the PhD course \href{https://www.unibz.it/en/faculties/engineering/phd-computer-science/study-course-offering/2025/36967}{\textit{Physics Informed Neural Network}, held at the University of Bozen/Bolzano} in the academic year 2025/2026.
  The goal of the course was to introduce the concept of Physics Informed Deep Neural Networks (PINN) and Neural Operators (NOs), discuss their implementation from scratch in PyTorch and using advanced ad-hoc developed open-source libraries such as NVIDia PhysicsNeMo to address real-world problems in various fields (engineering, physics, petroleum reservoir). We discuss recent topics such as Mixture-of-Models, Fourier Neural Operators, Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (PIKANs) and Fourier Neural Operators.

</details>


### [324] [The Automaton Underneath: The Additive Input Pathway Is a Parasitic Attractor for State Tracking in Householder Linear RNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18966)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文通过预注册的因果消融实验揭示：DeltaNet/DeltaProduct类线性RNN中，加性输入注入项 b_t = W_b e_t 是导致长度泛化失败的"寄生"通路——删除它后模型能精确学习自动机并在16倍训练长度上泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性RNN（如DeltaNet/DeltaProduct）理论上能表示硬状态跟踪自动机，但训练后的模型无法长度泛化。近期工作将这一差距归因于优化问题，却缺乏因果性解释。本文旨在提供一个因果解释。

Method: 在预注册的、架构内因果消融框架下，对比保留与删除加性输入注入项 b_t = W_b e_t 的同一模型。测试任务包括parity、S_4、A_5、非可解S_5词问题。提出并检验表示律：每token最小Householder因子数等于任务生成元在格式固定表示下的最大反射长度。另设两个预注册实验臂定位机制：(i)从已验证精确解出发观察Adam行为；(ii)检验拟合是否经由b_t路由，并用置零W_b进行推理测试。

Result: 含b_t时模型在长度32上拟合但在分布外崩溃（S_5位置512准确率0.20）。删除b_t后，同一架构学到精确自动机，在16倍训练长度上中位准确率1.00。表示律验证成立：parity=1, S_4=3, A_5/S_5=4；低于该值无法拟合。与DeltaProduct在n_h=2时的S_4/A_5对比表明该律是表示相对的。机制发现：(i)从W_b=0精确解出发，Adam增大加性通路并将模型拉离精确解，而-b控制组保持1.00；(ii)49个拟合种子在W_b:=0下仍保持域内拟合，且在律最小宽度下推理时置零W_b恢复精确泛化（parity 5/5, S_5 5/5, S_4 4/5, A_5 4/5）。共202次运行全部预注册。

Conclusion: 加性输入路径是"寄生"的：它先使模型失稳，随后掩盖已正确学到的自动机。任务格式是状态跟踪基准中的隐藏变量。该工作为线性RNN长度泛化失败提供了因果解释，并指出移除加性注入可能恢复正确自动机学习。

Abstract: Linear RNNs with input-dependent Householder-product transitions (DeltaNet/DeltaProduct-class) can provably represent hard state-tracking automata, yet trained models fail to length-generalize -- a gap recent work attributes to optimization, without a causal account. We give one, in a pre-registered, within-architecture causal ablation: the same model with one term deleted -- the additive input injection $b_t = W_b e_t$. With $b_t$, models fit length 32 and collapse out-of-distribution on parity, $S_4$, $A_5$, and non-solvable $S_5$ word problems (0.20 at position 512 on $S_5$). Without it -- input acting only through the orthogonal transitions -- the same architecture learns the exact automaton: median accuracy 1.00 at 16x the training length, at every width admitted by a representation law we state and test: the minimal number of Householder factors per token equals the maximal reflection length of the task's generators in the format-pinned representation (parity 1, $S_4$ 3, $A_5$ and $S_5$ 4); below it, nothing fits. The contrast with DeltaProduct's $S_4$/$A_5$ at $n_h{=}2$ (group-element classification, $SO(3)$ realization) shows the law is representation-relative: task format is a hidden variable in state-tracking benchmarks. Two pre-registered arms locate the mechanism. (i) Initialized at a verified-exact solution with $W_b{=}0$, Adam grows the additive path and pulls the model off the exact solution; $-b$ controls stay at 1.00. (ii) Our registered prediction that the fit routes through $b_t$ fired its kill criterion: all 49 fitting seeds retain in-domain fit under $W_b{:=}0$ -- and at law-minimal width, zeroing $W_b$ at inference restores exact generalization (parity 5/5, $S_5$ 5/5, $S_4$ 4/5, $A_5$ 4/5). The additive pathway is parasitic: it destabilizes, then conceals, a correctly learned automaton. All 202 runs pre-registered; all numbers regenerate from artifacts.

</details>


### [325] [Fathom: Per-Query Read Depth for Sparse Decoding over Offloaded KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17652)
*Vivek Kalyanarangan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fathom提出一种自适应比特的key扫描方法，用位平面存储4-bit K缓存，查询按通道重要性反向注水分配比特预算，在百万token下解码加速且误差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百万token下KV cache及索引存于host memory，对全部n个key扫描做top-k成为解码瓶颈；现有固定比特扫描（如136-bit）读取数据量过大。

Method: 将4-bit K cache以通道优先的位平面方式存储，前t个平面即该通道的t-bit量化器；查询根据方差加权通道重要性，通过反向注水（reverse water-filling）分配比特预算，决定每个key通道读取的位数。

Result: 百万token、Qwen3-8B下解码GPU时间比Double Sparsity/Loki/SparQ r=32的136-bit扫描快1.67x；与SparQ 68-bit读取等GPU时间下少读18%字节，且7个模型/上下文设置中6个注意力误差更低；RULER类任务每token扫描匹配精确top-k；真实编码agent会话中92-bit达到136-bit扫描的step agreement。

Conclusion: 存储复用已有量化栈的4-bit K副本，无需额外开销；但索引驻留GPU内存时无加速。

Abstract: When agentic sessions run to a million tokens with many sessions resident at once, the KV cache and the index that ranks it live in host memory, and the scan that ranks all n keys for a top-k step becomes the traffic that bounds decoding. We present Fathom, a key scan in which each query decides how many bits of each key channel to read. The 4-bit K cache is stored channel-major as bit planes, so a prefix of t planes is exactly the channel's t-bit quantizer, and the query spends its bit budget by reverse water-filling over the variance-weighted importance of its channels. At one million tokens on Qwen3-8B a decode step is 1.67x faster in GPU time than with the 136-bit scans of Double Sparsity, Loki and SparQ r=32, and in the same GPU time as SparQ's 68-bit read (r=16) Fathom reads 18% fewer bytes with lower attention error on six of seven model and context settings. On RULER-style tasks every per-token scan matches exact top-k decoding, and on real coding-agent sessions Fathom reaches the step agreement of the most accurate 136-bit scan at 92 bits. The store is the 4-bit K copy a quantized serving stack already holds, and the method is not faster when the index is resident in GPU memory.

</details>


### [326] [Reflect, Revise, Reuse: Training-Free Skill Evolution for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17653)
*Bofan Chen,Boxuan Zhang,Fei Tang,Zhengxi Lu,Yong Du,Tongbo Chen,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvoSkill-GUI：一种无需训练的GUI智能体技能自进化框架，通过reflect-revise-reuse循环在执行中修订技能，在三大基准上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI任务在动态界面上执行，弹窗、延迟加载、控件移位会破坏预先固定的计划。现有技能框架不针对GUI执行动态，且将技能视为部署前产出的静态产物，无法通过执行反馈改进。

Method: 提出EvoSkill-GUI，无需训练。每个技能是结构化多文件包（检索元数据、可执行计划、备用定位、失败恢复规则、无障碍工具、失败案例）。采用reflect-revise-reuse循环：执行器在rollout中即时修订，隔离critic在严格信息隔离下诊断失败轨迹，执行器通过受限工具接口编辑技能文件。

Result: 在MobileWorld、AndroidWorld、OSWorld三个跨移动与桌面平台的基准上，无需训练即稳定提升多个基座模型，最大增益分别为+16.2%、+6.0%、+10.5%；进化后的技能库可迁移受益于相关任务，而非从头重建。

Conclusion: GUI智能体需要的不是更好的静态技能，而是能在部署时从执行反馈中修订的技能；无训练的技能自进化是可行且有效的。

Abstract: GUI agents execute long-horizon tasks on dynamic graphical user interfaces, where pop-ups, delayed loads, and relocated widgets routinely invalidate plans fixed before execution. Recent agent-skill frameworks encapsulate reusable procedural knowledge to mitigate this, yet existing skill designs are largely developed without targeting GUI execution dynamics and treat skills as static artifacts produced before deployment rather than living procedural knowledge that improves through it. We argue that what GUI agents need is not better static skills, but skills that can be revised from execution feedback at deployment time, without additional training. We propose \textbf{EvoSkill-GUI}, a training-free framework in which each skill is a structured multi-file package containing retrieval metadata, executable plans, backup localization, failure-recovery rules, accessibility utilities, and failure cases. EvoSkill-GUI operates through a \textbf{\emph{reflect-revise-reuse}} loop: the executor performs instant in-rollout revisions, an isolated critic diagnoses failed trajectories under strict information isolation, and the executor edits specific skill files through a restricted tool interface. Across MobileWorld, AndroidWorld, and OSWorld, three mainstream GUI benchmarks spanning mobile and desktop platforms, EvoSkill-GUI consistently improves multiple base models without any training, with maximum gains of $+16.2\%$, $+6.0\%$, and $+10.5\%$ respectively, and evolved skill libraries continue to benefit related tasks rather than being rebuilt from scratch. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/EvoSkill-GUI.

</details>


### [327] [LIGE-GR: A Smooth Leap from Ranking to Generative Recommendation in the LLM Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18148)
*Venkat Srinivas,Chenzhang He,Sam Woodmansee,Shawn Lian,Wenjie Hu,Renjie Jiang,Ziheng Huang,Xinyuan Zhang,Zhihao Zheng,Zhuoran Yu,Rui Li,Lei Yuan,Ziwei Li,Jimmy Jia,Mert Terzihan,Ekrem Kocaguneli,Yiming Liao,Zhichen Zhao,Yue Yin,Yue Weng,Wanlin Ma,Xufeng Cai,Weimiao Wu,Yezhou Huang,Du Zhang,Yukun Ding,Aaron Johnston,Yueming Wang,Zhaojie Gong,Yuting Zhang,Serena Li,Adithya Ganesh,Boying Liu,Haichuan Yang,Xialu Li,Matt Ma,Qunshu Zhang,John Joshua Miller,Praveen Rathinavelu,Cheng Huang,Aadhar Sachdeva,Josh Karns,Andres Aaron Gutierrez,Neil Agarwal,Gustas Pladis,Vladimir Batygin,Gopal Ray,Aditya Priyadarshi,Shantanu Patil,Zhe Wang,Penny Pan,Yiping Han,Arun Singh,Guangdeng Liao,Bi Xue,Xinyao Hu,Yang Song,Yisong Song,Meihong Wang,Haotian Wu,Deepak Agarwal,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LIGE-GR，一个面向工业推荐系统的列表级生成与评估框架，将传统逐项推荐升级为生成式推荐，同时保持与现有模型、价值函数和服务基础设施兼容，并在 Instagram Reels 和 Facebook Video 上取得正向收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的成功为下一代推荐系统提供灵感，推荐与语言生成在结构上相似（都生成优化用户体验的有序序列），但如何将 LLM 范式融入成熟的工业推荐系统仍是开放问题。两个挑战：一是不清楚如何将序列级生成与优化引入推荐；二是现实推荐系统经过多年围绕产品、业务约束、服务基础设施和组织归属的定制，整体替换风险高且组织扰动大。

Method: 提出 LIGE-GR 列表级生成与评估推荐框架。不从头重建整个推荐栈，而是将现有逐点（pointwise）推荐系统泛化为列表级（listwise）生成系统，从而在保留现有模型、价值函数和服务基础设施兼容性的同时获得列表级优化能力。

Result: 在 Instagram Reels 和 Facebook Video 的短视频推荐场景验证：Instagram Reels 用户停留时长提升 1.14%，Facebook Video 提升 0.72%，且仅需适度的额外推理资源。

Conclusion: LIGE-GR 为成熟工业推荐系统提供了一条向生成式推荐演进的可行路径，在不重建整个系统的前提下实现列表级生成与优化，并在真实大规模场景中验证了收益。

Abstract: The remarkable success of large language models (LLMs) has provided important inspiration for the next generation of recommender systems. Structurally, recommendation and language generation share a similarity: both aim to produce an ordered sequence that optimizes the user's experience.
